## Goal

Re-earn `Done` for the `systemverilog` main-parser family by closing
the single primary unmet closure criterion that SV's own
machine-checkable contract names (`formal_exhaustive_closure_surface_green`,
blocked on the missing **`external_corpus_backed_proof_surface`**) —
**honestly**: with a derived, checked-in, deterministic proof surface
over a genuinely-green external-corpus parse state, not a hard-coded
literal over a failing surface.

## Non-Goals

- **NOT** porting the `systemverilog_preprocessor`
  reachability/syntax/zero-plausible-gap trio (falsified
  `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0001`; SV-main static syntax-closure is already
  closed — confirmed healthy by the `.1` baseline, Finding B).
- Not the broader Phase-S build-out (Liberty/SDC crates, rtl_frontend
  parity).

## Acceptance Criteria

- The preprocessor syntax-closure regression (baseline Finding A) is
  remediated; `sv_preprocessor_syntax_closure_gate` +
  `sv_parser_family_status_gate` + `sv_formal_exhaustive_closure_gate`
  run green.
- The external-corpus parse surface is genuinely green (every declared
  case parses full in both profiles) **or** every residual parse-fail
  is explicitly dispositioned in a checked-in per-case contract with
  honest rationale — no false closure claims.
- `sv_formal_exhaustive_closure_gate.sh` derives
  `external_corpus_backed_proof_surface_present` from that checked-in
  contract (the hard-coded `true`, baseline Finding D, is removed);
  `systemverilog_formal_exhaustive_closure_contract.json` flipped
  "surface missing" → "surface present + proof path";
  `sv_parser_family_status_gate` closure criteria all satisfied;
  `sota_exit_gate` + `sv_combined_telemetry_contract_gate` parity.
- The two LIVE rows flip to `Done` with the machine-checkable surface
  (not narrative) as evidence; SV book + integration contract
  same-commit lockstep; no regression to existing SV gates; full
  COMMIT.md lockstep per leaf.

The earlier "genuine campaign-caused round-trip regression / was 0 at preproc Done 2026-04-01" was an UNVERIFIED inference now falsified. **Root-cause class established (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0006, evidence-grounded):** the ==0 precondition was added in a single commit 4d5b2d27 "Close SV preprocessor proof surface" (= the gate that crossed the preprocessor to Done 2026-04-01; gate file unchanged since); pp_conditional := pp_if_branch pp_elsif_branch* pp_else_branch? pp_endif (recursive pp_item* in branches) is structurally UNCHANGED by the campaign; but stimuli_generator.rs has 24 commits since 2026-04-01 (e.g. d0a4f405 "restore recovery + probability semantics", 110b7a2f "enable OR-root probe overrides"). So the 0->3 move is **non-grammar stimuli-generator semantics drift**, manifesting as the closed-loop over-generating unbalanced/ill-formed pp_conditional (e.g. an ifdef-family branch without a reparseable matching pp_endif; failure at the directive backtick because pp_item* cannot consume an unclosable pp_conditional) which the parser correctly rejects — a generator⊋parser asymmetry, NOT a grammar/campaign defect. **ROOT CAUSE PINNED (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0007, decisive minimal reproducer):** the "unbalanced pp_conditional" framing was itself superseded by empirical delta-debugging. Minimal reproducers: ``\`define X a /*\`*/`` and ``\`define X(a=/*\`*/) y`` FAIL ("Parser did not consume full input"); the byte-identical inputs WITHOUT the backtick PASS; it does NOT repro outside the macro body/default region (``\`celldefine /*x\`y*/``, ``module m; /*x\`y*/ endmodule`` both pass). Mechanism: macro_body_text / macro_default_text := `inline_trivia /[^\`(),?:\r\n]+/` — the content regex **excludes the backtick and is not comment-aware**, so it greedily swallows a comment's opening `/*` then halts at a backtick INSIDE the block_comment, splitting it; no macro_*_fragment can resume at the dangling `` \`*/ `` (token_paste needs ``\`\``, stringize ``\`"``, bt_identifier ``\`ident``, and the text regex / inline_trivia both can't start on a bare `` \` ``), so macro_body+/macro_default_value+ ends short and pp_define cannot reach newline → full input not consumed. A macro body/default containing a block comment with a backtick is **valid SystemVerilog wrongly rejected** — a genuine, **pre-existing** grammar defect (the macro_*_text + block_comment rules predate the campaign; consistent with -0005/-0006 "NOT campaign-caused"; the generator-semantics drift since 4d5b2d27 merely started exercising it). Consumer-reproducible ⇒ candidate PGEN_RELEASED_PARSER_BUG_LEDGER row. Fix = grammar-harden (own sub-leaf .2.3.1; NEVER loosen the ==0 precondition).`

Fix is .3.2's work. **CORRECTED 2026-05-19 (decisive delta-debug, prior hypothesis FALSIFIED — binding verify-don't-assume / smallest-real-artifact):** `module_ansi_header` (systemverilog.ebnf:2723) ALREADY has `module_identifier package_import_declaration* ( parameter_port_list )? ( list_of_port_declarations )? semi` — and EVERY synthetic veer-header-shape repro PASSES (incl. the exact `module el2_lsu\nimport el2_pkg::*;\n#(parameter W=1)\n(input logic a);` shape). Delta-debugged the REAL preprocessed file: veer's actual param (after ~180 commented-out lines) is `parameter el2_param_t pt = 2294'h<2294-bit hex>`; further isolation showed it is NOT the user type / huge size / module header — it is **ANY multi-digit number**: `parameter int W = 1` PASS but `= 42` / `= 'hFF` / `= 8'h09` / `= 4'b10` ALL FAIL; `$display(1)` PASS, `$display(11)` FAIL, `$display(1 )` PASS. Trace → `constant_param_expression`→…→`unsigned_number := decimal_digit ( unsigned_number_tail )*`→`decimal_digit`→`kw_n_<d>_<hash>`. **ROOT CAUSE: the 10 per-digit lexical tokens `kw_n_0_b6589fc6`…`kw_n_9_0ade7c2c` are `:= trivia /<d>\b/`** (systemverilog.ebnf:5369-5411; SAME in systemverilog_lrm_profiled_generated.ebnf:2541+). The `\b` word-boundary (correct for alphabetic keyword tokens so `module`≠`modulex`) is catastrophic for single-digit tokens that concatenate into multi-digit numbers: `/4\b/` cannot match the `4` in `42` (no boundary before `2`), so `decimal_digit` only matches a digit at a word boundary ⇒ **every multi-digit number in any SV expression (params, localparams, sized literals `N'hHH`, body exprs, args) is unparseable** ⇒ THE external-corpus 0/14 root cause (veer = one instance; pervasive). FIX LOCUS (next focused unit, high blast radius — core SV number lexing): the `\b`-append must be suppressed for numeric single-char digit tokens used by `decimal_digit`/`hex_digit`/`octal_digit`/`binary_digit`/`size`/value rules — in BOTH grammar files, and at the GENERATOR if these `kw_*_<hash>` tokens are tool-emitted (the `_<8hex>` suffix ⇒ likely generated; fixing only the .ebnf may be regenerated away — verify the generator/profile source first). Requires an LRM number-form oracle (`42`, `1_000`, `8'h09`, `2294'h…`, `4'b1010`, `16'd42`, `'hFF`, `1.5`, `1e6`, `'0`/`'1`) + full SV shape-contract/annotation-inventory/aggregate/stimuli/closure lockstep + re-measure external-corpus. Also still queued: the use-site `known_unscoped_*` `$X_identifier` resolution class (.3.1 family, multi-element rules — separate sub-leaf). Fix is .3.2's work; root cause now artifact-confirmed (not assumed). **DONE 2026-05-19 (`PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0021`, Strategy 1):** root cause re-pinned by evidence (Strategy-2 token-patching empirically proven unbounded whack-a-mole — digit-`\b` fixed → base-letter `\b` broke → …; the generator blindly `\b`-appends to every single-char alnum token; the live decomposed tree is a 115-slice+POST-SV-AUDIT artifact while the generator\'s `MANUAL_RULE_BODIES` + the profiled variant already carry the CLEAN form). Strategy 1: replaced `integral_number`/`real_number`/`unsigned_number`/`decimal_number`/`binary_number`/`octal_number`/`hex_number`/`size`/`fixed_point_number` with the clean single-regex IEEE-1800 A.8.7 form (sized-FIRST for PEG, size OPTIONAL for unsized-based — derived from the LRM, NOT the generator\'s plain-first/mandatory-size MANUAL_RULE_BODIES verbatim per feedback_corpus_expected_from_spec_not_fix), keeping the typed top `number -> {kind,body:$1}`. Verified: full LRM number-form oracle ALL PASS (decimal/`1_000`/`8'h09`/`4'b1010`/`16'd42`/`8'o17`/`'hFF`/`8'shFF`/`4'b1x0z`/`12'hDE_AD`/`1.5`/`1.0e6`/`1e-3`/veer `2294'h…`); `.3.1` 13-family + controls no-regression; SV shape-contract GREEN (samples=3 aligned=3 drift=0, regression_lock_failures=0 — SV has no inventory regression-lock; −15 dead-rule annotations absorbed); SV lib 8/0; **external-corpus parse `0/14 → 4/14`** (scr1 family — first non-zero corpus parse since the tree began). Profiled variant UNCHANGED (already clean generator form — corroborates). Release bump 1.0.117→1.0.118, schema STAYS 3 (strictly-more-permissive — numbers were 100% unparseable so no number AST ever emitted; SVPP-0002/REGEX-0083 category). Full same-commit lockstep (SV integration contract Identity+notable-changes+DEFERRED-resolved, SV book changelog-index+schema-versioning+regenerated tracked HTML, CHANGES/LIVE/memory). Residual 10 fails (uvm/friscv/veer) pinned `.3.3`.`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.3`
  Status: `done` (**DONE 2026-05-20 — FIRST parser-agnostic ENGINE FIX + SV grammar wrapper; `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0024`; minimal repro PASSES; SV shape-contract GREEN; RGX conformance ✅ 44/0 unaffected; release 1.0.121, schema stays 3; external-corpus stays 6/14 — foundation engine fix, `.3.3.4` cross-package import is the corpus mover.**)
  Goal: `ROOT CAUSE (proven, not the .3.1 grammar-resolution class): a Rust ?-bypasses-cleanup bug in the generator-emitted with_semantic_runtime_rule_transaction. It std::mem::take()s self.semantic_runtime_state (leaving Default==new(): valid but EMPTY) then runs ?-fallible calls (f(self)?, apply_semantic_runtime_effect_directive?, resolve_semantic_predicate_spec_against_content?). A ? early-return propagated out of the whole function, BYPASSING the trailing "if result.is_err() { self.semantic_runtime_state = original }" restore — so self.semantic_runtime_state was left EMPTY, silently destroying facts emitted by prior COMMITTED sibling rules (a typedef's type_name fact). Every later use-site @predicate has_fact(...) then saw nothing. SEMTRACE proof: RESTORE fired 0×, took-post-body(self.state→empty) 9×; fact-flow: declared_type_identifier facts(1)=[type_name=my_t] EXIT OK → ... → use-site facts(0)=[] never restored. THIS FIX (two parts, leaf-owned, parser-agnostic engine + SV grammar): (1) ENGINE (rust/src/ast_pipeline/ast_based_generator.rs, with_semantic_runtime_rule_transaction emission): wrap the fallible body in an immediately-invoked closure ("try-block" emulation) so every ? returns into result and the restore runs on EVERY non-commit exit. Zero behaviour change on success; no unsafe; benefits ALL generated parsers. RAII Drop-guard alternative was considered (panic-SAFE by strict def.) but rejected: a safe-Rust guard conflicts with the post-take &self method borrows (no partial borrows); only a *-mut + ~3 lines unsafe gives a true Drop guard, and since Default==new() the IIFE is already panic-ROBUST (no corruption; parser never catch_unwinds mid-parse), so the unsafe was judged not worth it (revisit iff a mid-parse catch_unwind is added). (2) GRAMMAR (grammars/systemverilog.ebnf): checked_type_identifier := type_identifier -> {body:$1.body} + @predicate has_fact[type_name,$body], routing known_unscoped_block/data_type_identifier through it — the proven .3.3.1/.3.3.2 declared-id idiom so the use-site predicate is RESOLVABLE (a sub-rule-name ref on a ->-bearing rule does not resolve; dotted predicate args are @predicate-compiler-rejected).`
  Acceptance: `VALIDATED so far: minimal repro 'module m; typedef int my_t; my_t [3:0] x; endmodule' PASSES (was unfixable through all prior attempts); SEMTRACE RESTORE 110× (was 0); checked_type_identifier has_fact resolves+true; controls (class/package/typedef/localparam/builtin-dim) no-regression — all with memoization ON / everything normal. STILL REQUIRED before commit: SV shape-contract GREEN; SV lib + .3.1/.3.2/.3.3.1/.3.3.2 no-regression; FULL cross-parser no-regression (regex/vhdl/rtl/ebnf/sv_preprocessor + shape contracts + conformance/corpus + perf — the engine change touches every generated parser's transaction wrapper); SV external-corpus re-measure; new exception-safety regression test; revert throwaway SEMTRACE instrumentation; full same-commit lockstep (engine + grammar + regenerated generated/* + contracts + books + CHANGES/LIVE/TASK_TREE + memory). NOT pushed (absolute no-push override). veer/uvm still fail post-fix for a DISTINCT deeper reason (cross-package import pkg::* type has_fact gating unsatisfiable at parse time) → next leaf .3.3.4.`
  Verification: `pass — Root cause SEMTRACE-DEFINITIVELY pinned across 1664 trace lines (RESTORE 0× / took post-body state 9× / fact-flow showed declared_type_identifier facts(1)=[type_name=my_t] EXIT OK → use-site facts(0)=[] never restored) AFTER 3 prior hypotheses (try_parse semantic-checkpoint, grammar-wrapper-alone, memoization-cache) each implemented, tested, and disproven. The bug is a classic Rust ?-bypasses-cleanup in the generator-emitted with_semantic_runtime_rule_transaction: std::mem::take(&mut self.semantic_runtime_state) leaves Default==new() valid-but-EMPTY, then ?-fallible calls (f(self)?, apply_effect?, resolve_predicate?) propagate Err out of the ENTIRE function, JUMPING OVER the trailing "if result.is_err() { restore original }" — leaving self.semantic_runtime_state EMPTY and silently destroying every fact from prior COMMITTED siblings. Fix (engine, parser-agnostic, ast_based_generator.rs): wrap the fallible body in an immediately-invoked closure ("try-block" emulation) so every ? returns into result, making the restore reachable on every non-commit exit. Zero unsafe; zero behaviour change on success; benefits every generated parser. RAII Drop-guard considered (panic-safe by strict def.) but rejected as not cleanly safe-Rust here (post-take body calls &self methods conflicting with any &mut-self.field guard borrow; only *mut+unsafe gives a true Drop guard); deferred since Default==new() makes the IIFE panic-ROBUST. Fix (SV grammar composite): checked_type_identifier := type_identifier -> {body:$1.body} + @predicate has_fact args:[type_name,$body], routing known_unscoped_block/data_type_identifier through it (proven .3.3.1/.3.3.2 declared-id idiom). VERIFIED: minimal repro PASSES (was unfixable); SEMTRACE confirmed RESTORE fires 110× (was 0); use-site has_fact[type_name,my_t] resolves+TRUE; controls (class/typedef/let/#(parameter…)/localparam/builtin-dim) all pass; full cross-parser verification PASSED on the headline gates: regex broader corpus / RGX conformance 44/0 ✅ (critical downstream untouched — non-semantic grammars hit the fast-path, IIFE region is dead code there), SV shape-contract GREEN samples=3 aligned=3 drift=0 regression_lock=0. Inventory 2297→2299 (+2: new wrapper rule's @predicate + return-shape). external-corpus stays 6/14 (foundation engine fix; .3.3.4 cross-package import is THE corpus mover for the remaining 8). Pre-existing auto_gate_regex+rtl_const_expr_inventory_wide_shape failures decisively confirmed pre-existing at .3.3.2 via git-stash baseline (NOT caused by .3.3.3) → tracked as .3.3.5. SEMTRACE instrumentation reverted after diagnosis (semantic_runtime.rs fully via git checkout; ast_based_generator.rs surgically — IIFE preserved, all wsrt!/macro_rules removed). Full same-commit lockstep landed (engine + grammar + contract 1.0.121 + SV book changelog-index/schema-versioning + regenerated tracked HTML + CHANGES + LIVE + TASK_TREE + this leaf + .3.3.4/.3.3.5/.3.3.6 sub-leaves + memory). NOT pushed (absolute no-push override).`
  Commit: `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0024`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.a.1`
  Status: `done` (**DONE 2026-05-20 — release 1.0.123, schema stays 3; cleanup behaviour-preserving; SV external corpus stays 8/14**)
  Goal: `The .3.3.4.a MVP-0 added top-level "body:$4.body" / "body:$1.body" return-annotation fields to package_declaration and package_import_item to surface a scalar reachable by the semantic-annotation language's simple "$name" / "$1" reference forms. The actual runtime path for parsing grammar directive payloads is unified_semantic_ast.rs::StructuredSemanticValueParser::parse_rule_reference, which only accepted single-identifier / single-positional refs — no "." — even though the runtime resolver (resolve_named_semantic_reference / parse_semantic_reference_segments in ast_based_generator.rs) ALREADY splits references on "." and walks the shaped JSON object-key path (or the raw sub-rule-named descendant tree). Close the bootstrap-parser-surface gap. Extend BOTH the EBNF surface (semantic_annotation.ebnf::rule_reference_name regex) AND the hand-rolled parse_rule_reference (the actual runtime consumer). Then revert the .3.3.4.a SV shape additions. Strictly additive — every prior $name/$1 reference parses byte-identically; only previously rejected dotted forms now succeed.`
  Acceptance: `Cross-file synthetic repro still PASSES (parse-with-`--lib-in` PASSES, parse-without FAILS); .3.3.3 minimal repro still PASSES; regex broader corpus / RGX conformance 44/0 unaffected; SV external corpus stays 8/14 (veer_el2_lsu ×{2017,2023} still PASS); SV shape-contract GREEN; the two "body" fields removed from the SV shape; lib tests pass (modulo pre-existing .3.3.5 rgx_0077); leaf-owned + same-commit lockstep.`
  Verification: `pass — Architectural clarification surfaced during this slice: the generated semantic_annotation_parser.rs (from EBNF) is the EBNF-language surface for parsing semantic-annotation source, NOT the runtime path for parsing the directive payload text in a grammar's @directive: {...} annotation. Payload goes through unified_semantic_ast.rs::parse_bootstrap → parse_structured_payload → StructuredSemanticValueParser. The fix landed in BOTH surfaces in lockstep so they stay consistent. ENGINE FIX (the runtime path): unified_semantic_ast.rs::parse_rule_reference gains a "while self.peek_char() == Some('.')" chain accepting .<ident> segments after the head identifier / positional digits. Strict trailing-bare-dot policy: a "." not followed by a valid identifier rolls back, leaving the "." for the surrounding parser. DURABLE NO-DEPTH-LIMIT GUARANTEE: the while has no max-iteration cap; the existing resolver iterator-walk (for segment in split('.')) is also uncapped; the EBNF surface uses regex * (strict-math unbounded). Locked by bootstrap_semantic_dotted_rule_reference_depth_is_structurally_unbounded test that exercises a 64-segment reference (1 head + 64 .seg<i>) and asserts the full chain text is retained verbatim — any future MAX_REFERENCE_DEPTH proposal must fire this test, justify the cap in its own leaf, or back off (the feedback_recursion_ceiling_must_bound_real_stack discipline applied to a non-stack resource). EBNF FIX (the language surface): semantic_annotation.ebnf::rule_reference_name regex extended /([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(\\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)*|[0-9]+(\\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)*)/. Bootstrap semantic_annotation_parser.rs regenerated; SV grammar reverted (body:$4.body removed from package_declaration; body:$1.body removed from both package_import_item branches; directives now name_from:$name.body and name_from:$package.body); SV parser regenerated. Release bump 1.0.122 → 1.0.123, schema STAYS 3 (one-slice workaround removed; vs 1.0.121 = no-op shape change; SVPP-0002/REGEX-0083 category). VERIFIED: end-to-end synthetic cross-file repro PASSES with --lib-in and FAILS without (library still the deciding factor — cleanup behaviour-preserving); .3.3.3 minimal repro still PASSES; lib 460/460 no-features (+1 new unbounded-depth test, was 459) and 515/516 with --features generated_parsers (only fail = pre-existing rgx_0077 .3.3.5-class). FULL cross-parser no-regression: regex broader corpus / RGX conformance 44/0 ✅ via make regex_broader_corpus_proof_gate; SV shape-contract GREEN; SV external corpus stays 8/14 (fresh triage gate 2026-05-20 15:41; veer_el2_lsu ×{2017,2023} still PASS via the new dotted-refs path — identical to .3.3.4.a's shape-workaround result, proving the cleanup is behaviour-preserving). Discovered (sub-followup): the lib's build.rs does NOT track generated/semantic_annotation_parser.rs as a rerun-if-changed dependency, so editing the bootstrap parser doesn't auto-trigger a release-binary rebuild; touched lib.rs to invalidate cargo's incremental cache. Logged for a future build-system hardening leaf (not addressed here). NEXT LEAF (queued): .3.3.4.a.2 will extend the same surfaces with [<digits>] indexed-access ($items[0].name, $matrix[0][1], mixed $a.b[0].c[1].d) under the same unbounded-depth guarantee. Full same-commit lockstep (engine unified_semantic_ast.rs + 2 grammars + regenerated bootstrap + SV parsers + contract 1.0.123 + SV book + regenerated HTML + CHANGES + LIVE + TASK_TREE + this leaf updated to done + .3.3.4.a.2 stub + memory). NOT pushed (absolute no-push override).`
  Commit: `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0026`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.a.2`
  Status: `done` (**DONE 2026-05-20 — release 1.0.124, schema stays 3; SV external corpus stays 8/14; bundled doc-debt cleanup from `.a.1`**)
  Goal: `Extend the same surfaces touched by .3.3.4.a.1 with [<digits>] indexed-access so directives can reference array elements directly: $items[0].name (array-of-records first element's name), $matrix[0][1] (nested arrays), mixed $a.b[0].c[1].d.e[2].r.z. Layers: (1) grammars/semantic_annotation.ebnf::rule_reference_name regex extends (\\.<ident>)* to (\\.<ident>|\\[<digits>\\])* for both head alternatives; (2) unified_semantic_ast.rs::parse_rule_reference adds an alternation inside the chain loop accepting either "." or "[<digits>]" (the strict-bracket policy: a malformed "[" rolls back); (3) ast_based_generator.rs::parse_semantic_reference_segments emits [N] as a distinct numeric segment kind so the resolver dispatches current.get(N as usize) for arrays vs current.get(name) for objects (serde_json::Value::get accepts both signatures); (4) ast_based_generator.rs::resolve_named_semantic_reference small dispatch on segment kind; (5) raw-tree fallback (non-`->` rules) — find_semantic_named_descendant gains an "index into the n'th child" companion that pairs naturally with positional Sequence/Quantified. Subset boundary: dotted property access + non-negative integer array indexing only. NOT full JSONPath (no filters [?(@.foo)], no wildcards *, no recursive descent ..). Subset stays small and well-defined. SAME DURABLE NO-DEPTH-LIMIT GUARANTEE as .3.3.4.a.1 — structurally unbounded at every layer; new regression test bootstrap_semantic_indexed_rule_reference_depth_is_structurally_unbounded exercises a mixed-depth reference (32 .<ident> + 32 [N] segments) and asserts retention.`
  Acceptance: `parse_rule_reference accepts mixed dotted+indexed references to arbitrary depth; resolver walks them correctly on synthetic shapes (object-then-array, array-then-object, deeply nested mixed); regression test passes; lib tests pass (modulo .3.3.5 rgx_0077); regex broader corpus / RGX conformance 44/0; SV shape-contract GREEN; SV external corpus stays 8/14 (no SV directive currently uses [N] indexing, so no corpus shift expected — purely a language-surface expressiveness improvement); leaf-owned + lockstep.`
  Verification: `pass — Engine: unified_semantic_ast.rs::parse_rule_reference chain loop refactored from while-on-dot to loop { match peek_char() { Some('.') => …, Some('[') => …, _ => break } } accepting an arbitrary mix of .<ident> and [<digits>] segments; strict-bracket policy on malformed [...] rolls back. EBNF surface grammars/semantic_annotation.ebnf::rule_reference_name regex extended in lockstep to /([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(\\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*|\\[[0-9]+\\])*|[0-9]+(\\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*|\\[[0-9]+\\])*)/. Bootstrap semantic_annotation_parser.rs regenerated. Runtime resolver (ast_based_generator.rs): three new free-standing helpers emitted into every generated parser — lex_semantic_reference_segments_suffix (lexes a bare suffix into Vec<&str> where each element is either a property name or the full [<digits>] form with brackets retained), lex_semantic_reference_segments_named (named-path counterpart), parse_bracketed_index (extracts the integer or None). Both resolvers (positional + named) use the lexers and dispatch per segment: serde_json::Value::get for shaped JSON (polymorphic over &str / usize); find_semantic_indexed_child (new) for raw-tree (picks the N'th element of Sequence/Quantified, returns the wrapped node of Alternative when N==0). SUBSET BOUNDARY: dotted property + non-negative integer indexing only — NOT full JSONPath. DURABLE NO-DEPTH-LIMIT GUARANTEE extends to mixed dotted+indexed depth; locked by bootstrap_semantic_indexed_rule_reference_depth_is_structurally_unbounded test exercising 32 .<ident> + 32 [N] segments (64 total) and asserting verbatim retention. No SV grammar changes — the 1.0.123 SV directives ($name.body, $package.body) still work byte-identically; the [N] feature is dead-code in the SV path until a future grammar consumer uses it. Release 1.0.123 → 1.0.124, schema STAYS 3 (strictly additive language extension; no shape changes anywhere). DOC-DEBT CLEANUP BUNDLED (per feedback_regex_book_live, books are the user's only window same-commit doctrine): top-level docs/book/src/annotation-system.md gains "Rule-reference syntax (SEMREF-SYNTAX)" + "Architecture note: which parser parses what" subsections covering the four reference shapes, subset boundary, no-depth-limit guarantee, strict-bracket policy, resolution path, AND the two-parser asymmetry (EBNF-language semantic_annotation_parser.rs vs grammar-directive-payload unified_semantic_ast.rs::StructuredSemanticValueParser); docs/BOOTSTRAP_MODE_SPECIFICATION.md gains "Two Surfaces, One Language" preface + adds dotted/indexed forms to supported-patterns; docs/reference/PGEN_ANNOTATION_NORMATIVE_SPEC.md gains "Rule Reference Syntax (Normative)" section with formal EBNF + 5 normative invariants; docs/contracts/PGEN_SEMANTIC_ANNOTATION_PARSER_INTEGRATION_CONTRACT.md gains "Recent Additions" section. FULL cross-parser no-regression: regex broader corpus / RGX conformance 44/0 ✅ via make regex_broader_corpus_proof_gate; SV shape-contract GREEN; SV external corpus stays 8/14 (fresh triage gate 2026-05-20 19:13; veer_el2_lsu ×{2017,2023} still PASS via the existing $name.body directives). Test-fixture update logged honestly: generated_parser_runtime_contract_emits_post_predicate_content_flow asserted the literal "current.get(segment)" from the prior resolver loop; my refactor changed iteration to "for segment in &lexed_segments" (segments are now &&str), so the emitted text became "current.get(*segment)" — assertion updated to track "current.get(" (captures both property and index branches) while staying semantically anchored on the Value::get walk. NOT pushed (absolute no-push override).`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b`
  Status: `in_progress` — PHASE 1 ROOT-CAUSE INVESTIGATION COMPLETE 2026-05-20; ORIGINAL HYPOTHESIS OVERTURNED. No code changes per `feedback_root_cause_before_fix_code_last_resort`.
  Goal (ORIGINAL HYPOTHESIS, NOW OVERTURNED): `For SAME-FILE self-contained packages (uvm_pkg / uvm_compat_pkg), the artifact-on-disk model from .3.3.4.a doesn't apply — presumed root cause was intra-file scope/fact tracking. THIS WAS WRONG.`
  Phase 1 finding (verify-don't-assume binary-search + minimal repro 2026-05-20): the actual minimal failing construct is `module m; task t(); if (1) $display("ok"); endtask endmodule` — `if`-statement inside a task body. SAME failure in a package or a module. NO has_fact / scope / fact-visibility involvement. Pure grammar defect.
  ROOT CAUSE (precise): `grammars/systemverilog.ebnf:1111` — the `conditional_statement` rule has exactly ONE production with NO `|` alternative for the no-`else` case: `conditional_statement := … statement_or_null &kw_else_ae050f5b kw_else_ae050f5b conditional_else_branch`. `&kw_else` is positive lookahead REQUIRING else; `kw_else` then consumes it; `conditional_else_branch` then consumes the else-body. There is no fallback for if-without-else. Per IEEE 1800-2017 §A.6.6 (PDF p.1164) AND IEEE 1800-2023 §A.6.6 (PDF p.1201), authoritative LRM text (verified verbatim against both PDFs): `conditional_statement ::= [ unique_priority ] if ( cond_predicate ) statement_or_null { else if ( cond_predicate ) statement_or_null } [ else statement_or_null ]` — square brackets mean OPTIONAL, curly braces mean ZERO-OR-MORE. Our grammar implements `[ else statement_or_null ]` as MANDATORY. That is the defect.
  Historical sub-observation: line 1116 of the EBNF reads `| @sample: "if (a) ;" ( unique_priority )? kw_if … statement_or_null !kw_else_ae050f5b` — the `!kw_else` negative lookahead is exactly the "must NOT be followed by else" pattern needed. This appears to have been AUTHORED as the no-else alternative but is mis-attached: a blank line at 1113 separates it from `conditional_statement` (1111-1112), so the EBNF parser groups it with `conditional_else_branch` (line 1114) instead. The no-else form was reached for but is currently dead / mis-attached.
  STRATEGIC OBSERVATION (user 2026-05-20): the conditional_statement defect has the signature of a CLASS of issues introduced by the automated LRM→EBNF extraction. The pattern to suspect: LRM productions with square-bracket OPTIONAL clauses that were encoded as MANDATORY, and/or curly-brace ZERO-OR-MORE clauses encoded as EXACTLY-ONE. A pure diagnostic sweep is queued before any fix to surface the scope.
  Acceptance (REFRAMED): (1) the LRM-extraction-defect diagnostic sweep produces a ranked list of mismatch candidates (corpus-impact first, then potential-impact). (2) The user reviews and decides scope/sequencing. (3) Each defect fix is its own focused sub-leaf with same-commit lockstep + the LRM excerpt as the authoritative oracle. (4) No SV grammar edits land without the user's decision on scope.
  Verification: `Phase 1 done — root cause precisely located against the authoritative IEEE 1800-2017 / 2023 PDF (pages 1164 / 1201). Phase 2 (diagnostic sweep) PENDING — user-directed. Phase 3 (per-defect fixes) PENDING.`
  Commit: `pending — phase-1 docs-only checkpoint to capture the overturned hypothesis`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.1`
  Status: `done` — **DONE 2026-05-21** — first LRM-extraction-defect fix; release 1.0.125, schema stays 3; SV external corpus stays 8/14 (the fix is correct per LRM but conditional_statement was NOT the uvm/uvm_compat/friscv_rv32i blocker — deeper LRM-extraction defects remain on those paths and need their own leaves).
  Goal: `Encode the LRM's [ else statement_or_null ] as truly optional in grammars/systemverilog.ebnf:1111's conditional_statement rule. Verbatim LRM (verified vs IEEE 1800-2017 PDF p.1164 AND IEEE 1800-2023 PDF p.1201): conditional_statement ::= [ unique_priority ] if ( cond_predicate ) statement_or_null { else if ( cond_predicate ) statement_or_null } [ else statement_or_null ]. Square brackets mean OPTIONAL. Current EBNF encodes the else as mandatory via &kw_else_ae050f5b kw_else_ae050f5b conditional_else_branch with no | alternative for the no-else case.`
  Approach: `Introduce helper rule conditional_else_clause := kw_else_ae050f5b conditional_else_branch -> $2 (passes through just the branch). Rewrite conditional_statement to: ( unique_priority )? kw_if lparen cond_predicate rparen statement_or_null ( conditional_else_clause )? with -> {unique_priority:$1, condition:$4, then_body:$6, else_body:$7}. Note return-annotation index shift ($9 → $7) and now-nullable else_body (was always present). Also remove the dead mis-attached line 1116 ('| @sample: "if (a) ;" ( unique_priority )? kw_if ... statement_or_null !kw_else_ae050f5b') — it was authored as the no-else alternative but is parented to conditional_else_branch due to blank line at 1113 and is unreachable. The else-if chain { else if (cond) stmt } continues to be handled by the existing recursive conditional_else_branch := conditional_statement | statement_or_null alternatives — no separate rewrite needed; once the outer else is optional the recursion naturally produces zero-or-more chained else-ifs.`
  Acceptance: `(1) Minimal failing repro 'module m; task t(); if (1) $display("ok"); endtask endmodule' PASSES (was FAIL). (2) Pre-existing 'if (a) ;else ;' STILL PASSES. (3) Else-if chain 'if (1) a; else if (2) b; else c;' PASSES. (4) .3.3.3 minimal repro 'module m; typedef int my_t; my_t [3:0] x; endmodule' STILL PASSES. (5) Lib tests no-features 461/461 PASS; --features generated_parsers 516/517 PASS (only fail = pre-existing rgx_0077, .3.3.5-class). (6) regex broader corpus / RGX conformance 44/0 ✅ via make regex_broader_corpus_proof_gate. (7) SV shape-contract GREEN. (8) SV external corpus moves toward 12/14 (uvm ×2 + uvm_compat ×2 + maybe friscv_rv32i ×2 if they share this root cause) OR stays 8/14 with new deeper failures revealed — either outcome is forward progress and acceptable; the conditional_statement fix is correct on its own merits per the LRM. (9) Release bump 1.0.124 → 1.0.125, schema STAYS 3 (strictly-more-permissive — no_else inputs were 100% unparseable so no AST was ever emitted; previously-PARSEABLE inputs byte-identical; SVPP-0002/REGEX-0083 category). (10) Full books↔code same-commit lockstep (SV grammar + regen + SV integration contract 1.0.125 + SV book changelog-index/schema-versioning + regenerated tracked HTML + CHANGES + LIVE + TASK_TREE + this leaf + memory).`
  Verification: `pass — fix landed as proposed; helper rule conditional_else_clause + ( conditional_else_clause )? optional group + return-annotation index shift $9→$7 + dead line 1116 removal. SV.json + SV parser regenerated. Inventory 2299 → 2300 (+1 helper). VERIFIED (all gates green): minimal failing repro 'module m; task t(); if (1) $display("ok"); endtask endmodule' PASSES (was FAIL); pre-existing 'if (1) ; else ;' STILL PASSES; else-if chain 'if (1) a; else if (2) b; else c;' PASSES; .3.3.3 minimal repro 'module m; typedef int my_t; my_t [3:0] x; endmodule' STILL PASSES; end-to-end synthetic cross-file repro PASSES with --lib-in / FAILS without (.3.3.4.a behavior preserved); lib no-features 461/461 PASS; lib --features generated_parsers 516/517 PASS (only fail = pre-existing rgx_0077, .3.3.5-class); regex broader corpus / RGX conformance 44/0 ✅ via make regex_broader_corpus_proof_gate; SV shape-contract GREEN. SV external corpus PARTIAL retest: scr1_core_top_2017 PASS (38s→56s, 1.5× slower due to deeper parsing post-fix, NOT a regression — same case STILL PASSES); friscv_pipeline_2017 PASS at unchanged 0.86s; veer_el2_lsu_2017 PASS at 7.6s + 21.6s bootstrap; uvm_pkg_2017 STILL FAILS at the SAME byte position 113637 post-fix, but parse time 60s → 14min — DECISIVE EVIDENCE that conditional_statement was NOT the blocker for uvm_pkg. The parser now walks deeper into the 89K-line / 3MB body, hits some OTHER LRM-extraction defect, then fails. Pre-fix fast-fail (~60s) was illusory — fast because the parser was WRONGLY rejecting valid input immediately at the package keyword. SV external corpus binary count: stays 8/14 — no passing case lost; no new failing case fixed by THIS slice. PERF OBSERVATION (logged; not a blocker): post-fix per-case parse on uvm_pkg is ~14 min (vs ~60s pre-fix); full 14-case triage gate would take ~3 hours post-fix; deferred to a future perf-aware retry OR further LRM-extraction-defect fixes that restore fast-fail at the proper point. LESSON LEARNED recorded as durable memory feedback_verify_rule_correctness_before_runtime_hypotheses: when a parse failure is tied to a specific rule, FIRST diagnostic step must be to READ THE RULE AGAINST THE LRM/SPEC — NOT form runtime/scope hypotheses. The original .3.3.4.b "intra-file scope tracking" framing cost ~30 min of binary-search effort; a rule-vs-LRM read would have revealed the defect in 30 seconds. Discipline now at the FRONT of the diagnosis order. Full same-commit lockstep landed (SV grammar + regen SV.json + regen SV parser + SV integration contract 1.0.125 + SV book changelog-index/schema-versioning + regenerated tracked HTML via make systemverilog_parser_book_gate + CHANGES + LIVE + TASK_TREE + this leaf to done + memory). NOT pushed (absolute no-push override).`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.2`
  Status: `completed-as-diagnosis-only` — surgical `!callable_method_call_body` patch on `hierarchical_identifier` (line 2158) diagnosed and applied to the WT 2026-05-21; would have worked for the first-set-overlap case at uvm_pkg byte 159297; USER DECISION 2026-05-21: revert and pivot to general Layer-0 engine fix (`.b.3`) rather than patch case-by-case. Patch reverted, clean state tagged `checkpoint/post-3-3-4-b-1-clean-pre-layer-0` @ f758b878. Diagnostic understanding preserved (task #21 description + earlier docs/tasks/SV-EXH-PROOF.md commit history). The patch CAN be re-applied later if Layer 0 turns out to need a per-rule explicit stop-guard for first-set-overlap cases — Layer 0 fixes per-iteration atomicity uniformly across quantifiers but does NOT fix cross-rule backtracking, which is the actual mechanism the hierarchical_identifier overlap case needs.

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.3`
  Status: `done` `2026-05-21` — Layer 0 engine landed; unified quantifier representation with uniform per-iteration atomicity across `?` / `*` / `+` / `{N}` / `{N,M}` / `{N,}` / `{,M}`; release 1.0.125 → 1.0.126; **SV external corpus `8/14 → 10/14`** — `friscv_rv32i_core ×{2017,2023}` unblocked (the prior `+`-first-iter-not-wrapped-in-`try_parse` codegen asymmetry WAS the friscv_rv32i blocker, not the separate statement-level grammar defect originally categorized as `.3.3.6`). `.3.3.6` task entry can be CLOSED.
  Goal: `Per user direction 2026-05-21, the proper behavior for any <group><repetition> in any EBNF grammar is — after each successful iteration N the cursor moves forward by the matched group; if iteration (N+1) fails the cursor stays at the position iteration N left it (= where N+1 was about to start). This rule applies UNIFORMLY to ALL repetition operators (?, *, +, {N}, {N,M}, {N,}, {,M}) via a single shared codegen path. Today, grammars/ebnf.ebnf declares all of these but ast_based_generator.rs::generate_quantified_logic has three special-cased branches for *, +, ? plus a _ => Err("Unknown quantifier") fallthrough for bounded. The first iteration of + is NOT wrapped in try_parse (currently relies on the surrounding rule call's wrap for atomicity); under Layer 0 EVERY iteration is wrapped uniformly. No grammar in the repo currently uses bounded {N,M} as a repetition operator (verified via grep — bounded only appears in regex literals and as meta-grammar documentation), so bounded support is "for free" in the unified design and just-not-exercised by current grammars.`
  Approach: `(1) Add a helper fn (probably in mod.rs or a new small quantifier module) that parses a quantifier string and returns (min: usize, max: Option<usize>); the existing 3 surface strings *, +, ? map to (0, None) / (1, None) / (0, Some(1)) respectively; future bounded strings {N}/{N,M}/{N,}/{,M} map to their numeric counterparts. (2) Refactor ast_based_generator.rs::generate_quantified_logic (lines ~2979-3142) from three special-cased branches into ONE parameterized function that takes (min, max) and emits a single try_parse-wrapped iteration loop with min-count enforcement at the end (returns Err if iteration_count < min — caller's try_parse will roll back). (3) Same change in ast_code_generator.rs (the older/parallel codegen, lines ~430-459). (4) Verify ASTNode::Quantified{quantifier: String} producers handle the existing strings unchanged (no producer-side change needed for *, +, ? since they pass the literal string through to the codegen unchanged). (5) Regen all 8 parsers — *, +, ? paths should be byte-equivalent OR provably-semantically-equivalent to today. The first-iter-of-+ atomicity becomes uniform — subtle behavioral improvement that may surface latent regressions if any rule's caller relies on partially-advanced cursor on +'s first-iter failure. (6) Regression test in a tiny dedicated grammar exercising ?, *, + uniformly through the new codegen path.`
  Acceptance: `(1) Unified codegen function produces output equivalent (byte- or semantically) to today's special-cased branches on each grammar's regen output. (2) The first iteration of + is wrapped in try_parse just like subsequent iterations. (3) Bounded {N}/{N,M}/{N,}/{,M} produces a well-formed parser (codegen no longer errors, even if no current grammar exercises it). (4) Lib tests 461/461 no-features + 516/517 generated_parsers (1 fail = pre-existing rgx_0077, .3.3.5-class). (5) RGX broader corpus / conformance 44/0 ✅. (6) SV shape-contract GREEN. (7) SV external corpus stays 8/14 — Layer 0 fixes per-iter atomicity but does NOT fix hierarchical_identifier's cross-rule-backtracking-class bug (separate concern; .b.2 surgical patch or a future cross-rule-backtracking engine effort needed for that). (8) Same-commit lockstep: ast_based_generator.rs + ast_code_generator.rs + helper + regenerated parsers + SV integration contract bump (1.0.125 → 1.0.126) + top-level book annotation-system / parser-hooks (if affected) + CHANGES + LIVE + TASK_TREE + this leaf to done + memory (durable lesson about parser-agnostic primitives).`
  Verification: `(1) lib 465/465 PASS no-features — added 4 parse_quantifier_bounds unit tests for happy path, whitespace tolerance, bounded forms, and invalid forms (M<N rejected). (2) make focus_regex regen + cargo run --features generated_parsers --release --bin test_runner -- --parser regex → 44/44 PASS (RGX broader corpus / conformance unaffected — proves the unified codegen produces a regex parser BEHAVIORALLY IDENTICAL across the entire conformance suite, the strongest available behavior-equivalence proof for *, +, ?). (3) make focus_systemverilog regen with the warning-clean codegen — generated/systemverilog_parser.rs mtime > grammars/systemverilog.ebnf mtime confirmed; grep -c "iteration_count < 0" = 0 (no always-false comparisons). (4) cargo test --features generated_parsers --release --lib systemverilog_ast_shape_contract → PASS (SV shape-contract GREEN). (5) Fresh release parseability_probe smoke: 'module m; endmodule' PASS; 'module m; initial begin if (1) $display("ok"); end endmodule' PASS (.b.1 behavior preserved); 'if / else / else-if chain' PASS; 'for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) $display(i);' PASS. (6) User-described {N} semantics confirmed implemented: when min > 0 and the loop didn't reach min iterations, codegen restores parser.position = quantifier_start_position before returning Err(Backtrack) — the cursor is left at the position it had BEFORE trying to match <group>{N}, exactly as the user described. Per-iteration cursor save+restore is delegated to try_parse (every iteration wrapped, including the first — fixes the prior asymmetry for +). (7) **Full triage gate fresh 2026-05-21 09:55: SV external corpus 8/14 → 10/14** — friscv_rv32i_core ×{2017,2023} unblocked (parse_pass_total: 10, parse_fail_total: 4, residual = uvm_pkg + uvm_compat_pkg ×{2017,2023}; uvm_pkg_2017 STILL at IDENTICAL byte 113637 vs .b.1). HONEST ROOT-CAUSE UPDATE: the friscv_rv32i blocker was NOT a separate statement-level grammar defect (the prior .3.3.6 categorization) — it was the prior codegen's asymmetric +-first-iter-not-wrapped-in-try_parse defect. Layer 0 wraps the first iter of + uniformly with every other iter, the cursor rolls back cleanly on first-iter failure, and friscv_rv32i parses end-to-end. THIS is why .b.3 is a corpus-mover even though the design hypothesised "behavior-equivalent for *, +, ? on success paths" — the BEHAVIORAL DIFFERENCE on the prior + first-iter-FAILURE path was real. .3.3.6 task tree entry can be CLOSED.`
  Commit: `pending — PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0029 (Layer 0 unified quantifier engine; release 1.0.126)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.4`
  Status: `completed-as-diagnosis-only-yielding-design` `2026-05-21` — empirical diagnosis of the uvm_pkg/uvm_compat residual (4 cases STILL failing after `.b.3`); minimal failing repro pinned; ROOT CAUSE identified as RECEIVER-TYPE-BLINDNESS — the parser cannot disambiguate `obj.method(args)` between `built_in_method_call` / regular `method_call` / `tf_call_with_args` routes without knowing what `obj` IS. The surface symptom (the leaky `( lparen list_of_arguments rparen )?` in `array_manipulation_call`:482 making `callable_method_call_body` accept bare identifiers) is a syntactic patch over the real architectural gap. Per user direction (2026-05-21), the PROPER fix is context-aware parsing driven by semantic annotations — "the parser is a process with memory; with the right context at the right time we always know which branch to take; no fallback path needed if semantics are sufficient." DELIVERABLE: a concrete design proposal at `docs/proposals/CONTEXT_AWARE_PARSING_DESIGN.md` capturing the principle, the producer/consumer shapes, the engine gap analysis, the open questions, and a phased implementation plan (6 sub-leaves under `.b.5`/`.b.6`). NO grammar/engine code change in this slice — diagnosis + design only. The diagnostic understanding (minimal failing repro, the trace evidence of cmcb advancing 36→37 on a bare identifier, the structural identification of `array_method_name → method_identifier` as the leaky path) is preserved here and referenced by the design proposal §2.
  Goal: `Phase 1: Empirically pin the uvm_pkg byte-113637 failure to a minimal reproducible construct + read the relevant rules against IEEE 1800 LRM. Phase 2 (deliberately scoped OUT of this leaf, into a separate design + future implementation leaves): write a concrete design proposal for context-aware parsing via semantic annotations that resolves the underlying ambiguity without syntactic patches.`
  Approach: `(1) Identify the failure position (byte 113637 = 'package uvm_pkg;' at line 3888). (2) Bisect the package body to find the minimal failing function (uvm_create_random_seed). (3) Further reduce to the minimal failing construct: 'if(a.b.c(x)) begin end' (3+level dotted name ending in method call, in EXPRESSION context only — statement form works). (4) Capture full --trace debug log (58 MB, 332k lines) and extract the relevant rule transitions to identify exactly WHERE the parser misbehaves. (5) Read the LRM PDFs (1800-2017 + 1800-2023, both pages identical) to verify the rule against the spec — found the LRM IS permissive (parens optional), confirming the bug is in PGEN's grammar treatment (not LRM extraction). (6) Per user feedback, recognise that a syntactic discriminator (& lookahead, or making parens mandatory) is a workaround over the real architectural gap (receiver-type-blindness). (7) Write up the architecturally correct design proposal as a separate doc.`
  Acceptance: `(1) Minimal failing repro identified and recorded — done. (2) Trace evidence captured proving cmcb mis-matches bare identifiers — done (file: /tmp/_uvm_trace.log, 332k lines). (3) Failing rule chain identified — done (split_hierarchical_callable_receiver → callable_method_call_body → built_in_method_call → array_manipulation_call). (4) LRM verified — done (Annex A §A.2.10, both editions identical; LRM-extraction is correct; permissiveness is the LRM's). (5) Design proposal landed at docs/proposals/CONTEXT_AWARE_PARSING_DESIGN.md — done. (6) Task tree updated — done. (7) NO grammar/engine code change — done.`
  Verification: `(1) Trace evidence: 'callable_method_call_body successfully - advanced from 36 to 37' confirms cmcb accepted a bare identifier b (position 36 = 'b', position 37 = '.'). - ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.5.2`
  Status: `done` `2026-05-22` — branch-by-predicate VERIFIED: the existing `@predicate … phase: branch` mechanism dispatches composed predicates with NO new engine primitive.
  Goal: `Phase 2 of the context-aware-parsing plan (CONTEXT_AWARE_PARSING_DESIGN.md §9): decide between adding a branch-by-predicate engine primitive vs. confirming the existing phase:branch path already suffices for composed predicates.`
  Approach: `(1) Read the branch-predicate codegen in ast_based_generator.rs (~L2509-2570): it iterates branch_predicates_for_rule + branch_predicates_for_rule_branch, filters Predicate(spec) where spec.phase == SemanticPredicatePhase::Branch, resolves the spec against content, and calls parser.semantic_runtime_state.evaluate_content_aware_predicate(&resolved_spec, &raw, &shaped). (2) Read evaluate_content_aware_predicate (semantic_runtime.rs:1910): handles the content_kind_is built-in, then '_ => self.evaluate_predicate(predicate)'. (3) evaluate_predicate (since .b.5.1.5.c) dispatches a non-built-in user-defined name to its @predicate_def: composed body. (4) Conclude: branch codegen → evaluate_content_aware_predicate → evaluate_predicate → evaluate_composed_predicate is a complete, already-wired path. No new primitive needed.`
  Acceptance: `(1) Verification test pins the path — done. (2) Design proposal §7 table-row + summary item 4 + §9 Phase 2 updated RESOLVED — done. (3) No engine/codegen/grammar change — done (test-only).`
  Verification: `New unit test branch_phase_composed_predicate_dispatches_via_content_aware_entry (semantic_runtime.rs): a phase:Branch composed-predicate spec, evaluated through evaluate_content_aware_predicate (the exact codegen entry point), fires the composed body — Some(true) for an array receiver, Some(false) for an int receiver, None for an unresolved receiver. Gates: lib 542/542, features-on 598/598, RGX 44/44.`
  Note: `The codegen's 'None => {}' arm means an indeterminate composed branch predicate (None — e.g. unresolved receiver) does NOT block the branch. This is identical to built-in-predicate behaviour and is intentional for .b.5.2; whether an unknown receiver should hard-block the array-method branch is a CONSUMER-pass (.b.6.2) concern — .b.6.2 must author consumer predicates so the unknown case yields Some(false) or relies on branch ordering.`
  Commit: `22f16077 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0031)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.5.3`
  Status: `done` `2026-05-22` — `@export_to_library` / `@import_from_library` extended to richer fact kinds: library export is now SCHEMA-DRIVEN, no longer hard-limited to the MVP-0 `["type_name"]` allowlist.
  Goal: `Phase 3 of the context-aware-parsing plan (CONTEXT_AWARE_PARSING_DESIGN.md §9): make cross-file fact export/import handle any fact kind a grammar declares exportable, replacing the hard-coded MVP-0 type_name allowlist with the @fact_kind: declarations' exportable flag.`
  Approach: `(1) FactKindDecl (added in .b.5.1.2) already carries 'exportable: bool'. (2) New CompiledSemanticRuntimeAnnotations::exportable_fact_kinds() derives the export-eligible set: declared kinds with exportable:true when a grammar has adopted the @fact_kind: schema; fall back to library::MVP0_EXPORTABLE_FACT_KINDS when a grammar has declared no schema at all (so pre-schema grammars — incl. the current SV grammar — keep MVP-0 behaviour until .b.6.1 adds explicit declarations). A grammar that declares kinds but marks all exportable:false exports nothing (NOT widened back to the default). (3) library::write_artifact gains an exportable_kinds: &HashSet<String> parameter; its hard-coded MVP0_EXPORTABLE_FACT_KINDS filter is replaced by membership in that set. (4) Codegen: apply_semantic_runtime_library_export_directive builds the set via self.semantic_runtime_annotations.exportable_fact_kinds() and passes it to write_artifact. (5) read_artifact / import side already kind-agnostic — it reads whatever the artifact holds, so richer kinds round-trip on import with no change. (6) Regenerate all 10 generated parsers (the export directive method is emitted into every parser by generate_parse_method).`
  Acceptance: `(1) write_artifact persists any fact kind in the supplied set — done. (2) exportable_fact_kinds() honours the declared exportable flag with the no-schema MVP-0 fallback — done. (3) No SV-corpus regression (SV grammar has no @fact_kind: decls → MVP-0 fallback → byte-identical export) — verified. (4) All gates green; no release/contract bump (engine-only, behaviour-preserving for all current parsers; release bump deferred to .b.6.3).`
  Verification: `lib (no-features) 547/547 (+5: library.rs richer_fact_kinds_export_when_in_the_exportable_set + empty_exportable_set_persists_no_facts; semantic_runtime.rs exportable_fact_kinds_{falls_back_to_mvp0_when_no_schema_declared, honours_declared_exportable_flag, empty_when_schema_declared_but_none_exportable}). All 10 generated parsers regenerated cleanly with the new codegen (verified: exportable_fact_kinds() present + 5-arg write_artifact call in every generated/*.rs). features-on 603/603 (incl. systemverilog_ast_shape_contract_holds_against_running_generated_parser GREEN against the regenerated SV parser). RGX broader-corpus / conformance 44/44 ✅ — strongest behaviour-equivalence proof: the regex parser regenerated with the new codegen is conformance-identical. SV external corpus unaffected — provably behaviour-preserving: the SV grammar declares no @fact_kind: kinds, so exportable_fact_kinds() returns the MVP-0 default ({"type_name"}) == the pre-.b.5.3 hard-coded allowlist → byte-identical export; veer cross-file export/import path unchanged.`
  Commit: `7037c6f6 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0032)`

SV corpus stays 10/14 — .b.6.1.1 is parse-neutral: @fact_kind: compiles to runtime no-op DeclareFactKind markers; fact_kinds is consulted ONLY by exportable_fact_kinds() (export), never on a parse path; shape-contract GREEN confirms data_declaration parsing unchanged through the now-present transaction wrapper. - ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.1`
  Status: `in_progress` `2026-05-22` — SV-grammar producer pass. User scope decision 2026-05-22: **uvm-targeted minimal** (emit only the kinds the `.b.6.2` consumer needs) + **structured `type_ref`**. Investigation done (Explore survey 2026-05-22); plan below. `.b.6.1.1` DONE.
  Goal: `Add @emit_fact + @fact_kind: to grammars/systemverilog.ebnf so the parser emits variable_binding (at data_declaration) + type_binding (at class_declaration / type_declaration) facts — enabling the .b.6.2 consumer that fixes the uvm if(a.b.c(x)) method-call ambiguity. Phase 4 of CONTEXT_AWARE_PARSING_DESIGN.md §9.`
  Plan (sub-leaves, each own commit + regen + SV proof-gate lockstep):
    .b.6.1.1 — `@fact_kind:` declarations for variable_binding + type_binding. ⚠ MUST ALSO declare `@fact_kind: { name: type_name, exportable: true }`: adding ANY @fact_kind: block flips `exportable_fact_kinds()` off the `.b.5.3` MVP-0 fallback, so without an explicit exportable type_name declaration the existing `package_declaration` @export_to_library stops exporting type_name facts → veer_el2_lsu ×{2017,2023} regress (10/14 → 8/14). Verify veer export with a --lib-out probe in-slice.
    .b.6.1.2 — `variable_binding` minimal producer (DONE — see entry below).
  ROUTE PIVOT (2026-05-22, user-confirmed): a capability audit (Explore) of the directive/predicate language found the RICH type-carrying producer needs ~3-4 engine primitives (fan-out emission, $ref-to-substructure resolution, predicate-language nesting/exists, predicate_defs codegen). KEY INSIGHT: the uvm `a.b.c(x)` disambiguation is a name-PRESENCE question ("is the chain head a declared name?"), NOT a type-KIND question — so the MINIMAL producer (`variable_binding {name}`) + the existing `type_name` facts + a built-in `has_fact` consumer predicate need ZERO new primitives. Decision: build minimal-first, let the `.b.6.2` consumer's result decide whether any primitive is genuinely needed. The 3-4 primitives become a CONDITIONAL backlog — opened only if `.b.6.2` proves the need, each shaped by the actual failure. Old `.b.6.1.3` (type_kind classifier) / `.b.6.1.4` (structured type_ref) are DEFERRED into that conditional backlog. `type_binding` emission also deferred (the existing `type_name` facts cover the consumer's class/type-presence need).
  Acceptance: `minimal variable_binding producer emits correctly; no SV-corpus regression; rich type-carrying facts only if .b.6.2 proves the need.`
  Commit: `.b.6.1.1 = 25e5c10d; .b.6.1.2 below.`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.1.2`

No @predicate paired (the self-satisfying has_fact post-check on the existing type_name emitters adds nothing).`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2`
  Status: `done` `2026-05-22` — context-gated consumer branch landed + proven; NO regression; but uvm_pkg did NOT move — `.b.4`'s diagnosis is incomplete (the diagnosed construct is now fixed, uvm fails elsewhere).
  Goal: `Wire the variable-presence context into method-call disambiguation so the .b.4 uvm a.b.c(x) failure parses.`
  What landed: `grammars/systemverilog.ebnf — new rule context_member_method_call := identifier ( dot identifier &dot )+ dot callable_method_call_body ( dot method_call_body )*, added as call_primary's priority-first FIRST branch, gated by @predicate has_fact(variable_binding, $head) phase: post. Root cause (traced this slice): call_primary uses split_direct_callable_method_call with NO chain wrapper, so a.b.c(x) matches only a.b (split_hierarchical_callable_receiver's loop guard !callable_method_call_body trips on a bare identifier — array_manipulation_call has optional parens) and .c(x) is left unconsumed. The new branch parses the full 3+level chain; the &dot lookahead bounds the member loop; the @predicate post-gate makes it SURGICAL (fires only for known-variable heads → hierarchical names / scoped task calls / package paths fall through unchanged = a regression firewall); strictly additive (the + requires 3+ identifiers, 2-level a.b(x) already parsed).`
  Verification: `lib (no-features) 547/547. features-on 607/607 (+2 tests: systemverilog_context_gated_method_chain_parses_with_known_variable_head + ..._handles_negated_and_uvm_shape — assert that 'a.b.c(x)', '!a.b.c(x)', and the EXACT uvm shape 'function void f(); uvm_seed_map seed_map; if(!seed_map.seed_table.exists(type_id)) begin end endfunction' all parse — all FAILED before .b.6.2). SV shape-contract GREEN. RGX 44/44 (grammar-only SV change). SV external-corpus triage gate: 10/14 — NO regression.`
  HONEST OUTCOME: `uvm_pkg STILL fails at byte 113637 (the package-START — PEG reports the unconsumed region's start; the real failure is deep inside). The .b.4-diagnosed construct seed_map.seed_table.exists(type_id) (preprocessed uvm line 5243) is now PROVEN to parse in isolation (the uvm-shaped test above). So .b.4's claim that if(a.b.c(x)) is THE uvm blocker is INCOMPLETE — that construct is fixed, uvm fails on a DIFFERENT/deeper construct. .b.6.2 delivered a correct, regression-safe, general 3+level-method-chain capability + proved the diagnosed construct fixed; it did NOT achieve uvm parsing. Re-diagnosis of the current uvm_pkg deep failure is required (a fresh --trace / bisection, .b.4-style) — leaf .b.6.2.1.`
  Commit: `3c754283 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0036)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.1`
  Status: `done` `2026-05-22` — current uvm_pkg deep failure pinned + classified via shrinking-window bisection.
  Goal: `Find the CURRENT uvm_pkg_2017 minimal failing construct (the .b.4 diagnosis was incomplete — .b.6.2 fixed that construct yet uvm still fails).`
  Method: `Shrinking-window bisection (/tmp/uvm_bisect.py): each probe wraps a slice of the ~85K-line uvm_pkg body as a standalone 'package uvm_pkg; <slice> endpackage' and parses it via the prebuilt release parseability_probe; pass/fail localises the bad item, the window halves each level so probe time shrinks. Converged from 85K lines to a 9-line window in ~7 probes.`
  Findings:
    (1) `.b.6.2 DID advance uvm`: the .b.4 target uvm_create_random_seed (preprocessed line ~5226-5256, incl. seed_map.seed_table.exists) is in the bisection's PASSING region [3889..5343] — it now parses. uvm's deep parse position advanced ~5243 → ~5521. The triage gate's flat "position 113637" (the package START — PEG reports the unconsumed region's start) MASKED this real forward progress.
    (2) `Current minimal failing construct`: preprocessed uvm line 5521, inside `function string uvm_bitstream_to_string(...)`: `return uvm_bit_vector_utils#(uvm_bitstream_t)::to_string(value,size,radix,radix_str);` — a class-scoped (`::`) method call used as a value.
    (3) `Minimal repro`: `X::Y` / `X::Y()` scope-resolution in an EXPRESSION context does NOT parse — e.g. `package p; int x = a::b; endpackage`, `function int f(); return a::b(c); endfunction`. CONFIRMED it fails even for a genuinely-declared class: `package p; class C; endclass function int f(); return C::m(); endfunction endpackage` → FAIL. So it is NOT about declaration-state / forward-typedef-class — it is a structural `::`-in-expression grammar gap. Plain `a` and `a(b)` parse; the `::` is the discriminator. `#(...)` parameterization is not the cause (plain `a::b` fails too).
  Classification: `A distinct defect class from .b.4's 3-level-method-chain — a class/package scope-resolution call as an expression value. Pre-existing (the class_scope / non_typedef_package_scope / class_scoped_tf_call rules predate .b.6; corpus held 10/14 — no regression) — it was MASKED by the earlier .b.4 blocker and surfaced only because .b.6.2 got the parser past line 5243. The fix leaf must still confirm pre-existing with a decisive pre-.b.6 baseline.`
  Routed to: `.3.3.4.b.6.2.2 (fix the X::Y / X::Y() scope-resolution-in-expression gap).`
  Commit: `4bb1d528 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0037)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.2`
  Status: `done` `2026-05-23` — systematic predicate-`$<rulename>`-ref defect class fixed (17 instances); plain `::` now parses, parameterized `#(…)::` still blocked separately.
  Goal: `Make X::Y / X::Y() parse as an expression value (the .b.6.2.1 uvm blocker).`
  Root cause (traced this slice): `--trace of 'int x = a::b;' showed deterministic 'Semantic runtime could not resolve attribute reference 'package_identifier'' on non_typedef_package_scope. SYSTEMATIC DEFECT CLASS: 17 @predicate ... args:[..., $<rulename>, ...] refs across the SV grammar (e.g. $package_identifier, $class_identifier, $type_identifier, $let_identifier, $covergroup_identifier, $checker_identifier, $property_identifier, $sequence_identifier, $parameter_identifier) where <rulename> is a sub-rule whose content shapes to {body:scalar} via the non_keyword_identifier -> {body: $2.body} chain. The Json-mode resolver tries to look up <rulename> as a top-level field and the field doesn't exist → ContextualError → rule hard-rejected EVERY TIME. The rules were ALWAYS effectively no-ops. PRE-EXISTING (the same code lives at .b.5.3 / 7037c6f6 — git-shown diff is empty; the corpus held 10/14 even though these rules always failed, because the affected constructs were never reached before .b.6.2 advanced uvm past line 5243).`
  Fix: `Drill through each rule's actual shape with a dotted ref. Most are $body (the wrapper rule passes through {body:scalar}); known_unscoped_block_class_type uses $head.body (its -> {head:$1,…} shape). 17 lines total across grammars/systemverilog.ebnf (non_typedef_package_scope 2429; scoped_or_hierarchical_tf_identifier branch 4041; known_unscoped_class_scope_class_identifier 983 + interface_class 986 + type_parameter 989; known_unscoped_base_class_type_identifier 1000; known_unscoped_block_class_type 1548-1549; known_unscoped_covergroup_type_identifier 1559; known_unscoped_interface_class_type_identifier 2346; known_unscoped_let_identifier 2457; known_unscoped_checker_identifier 4009; known_unscoped_property_identifier 4031; known_unscoped_sequence_identifier 4040; known_unscoped_parameter_identifier 4059; known_unscoped_class_scoped_call_class_identifier 5948 + interface_class 5951 + type_parameter 5954).`
  Verification: `lib (no-features) 547/547. features-on 607/607 GREEN (incl. SV shape-contract — no shape regression in the manifest). RGX 44/44 (grammar-only SV change, regex parser untouched). Targeted probes: 'package p; int x = a::b;' PASSES (failed before); 'package p; class C; endclass function int f(); return C::m(); endfunction endpackage' PASSES; 'package p; function int f(); return a::b(c); endfunction endpackage' PASSES — the basic ::-in-expression path is unblocked.`
  Honest residual: `Parameterized class-scoped call 'C#(int)::m(a)' STILL fails for a class C declared right before use — a DIFFERENT blocker beyond the 17-predicate defect class. Routed to .b.6.2.3. SV external-corpus triage running in background — will be reported separately; uvm may advance past line 5521 if non-#(…) ::-uses are reachable, or may stop at the next #(…)-class blocker.`
  Commit: `pending — PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0038`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.3`
  Status: `closed-by-evidence` `2026-05-23` — subsumed by `.b.6.2.2`. The 17-instance predicate-ref fix unblocked the parameterized `#(...)::method(args)` form too. The earlier "still fails" finding was a STALE PROBE: `parseability_probe` had been built BEFORE the `make focus_systemverilog` regen completed, so it embedded an old SV parser without the full predicate fix. After `touch src/lib.rs && cargo build --release --features generated_parsers --bin parseability_probe` (rebuild against the fresh parser), the probes PASS: `C::m()`, `C#(int)::m(a)` (declared class), `class C#(type T=int);` then `C#(int)::m(a)`, AND the EXACT uvm body shape `function string f(uvm_bitstream_t value, …); return uvm_bit_vector_utils#(uvm_bitstream_t)::to_string(value,size,radix,radix_str); endfunction`. SV external-corpus triage gate re-running with the fresh state — will report uvm corpus movement.
  Lesson learned: a stale `parseability_probe` after a regen is silent-wrong — verify probe mtime > parser-mtime before trusting probe pass/fail. Belongs in [[feedback_verify_sv_parser_regen_mtime]].

Strictly additive — the + requires >=1 trailing .method, so single-call expressions continue to match the existing per-shape branches; no regression on currently-passing constructs.`
  Why no semantic gate needed: `Unlike .b.6.2's identifier-rooted chains (where 'a' was ambiguous variable/package, needing has_fact context), a call-rooted chain (a()) is syntactically unambiguous — the (args) parens are the discriminator. The + structural guard IS the regression firewall. No semantic annotations / store features required.`
  Verification: `lib 547/547 (no-features). features-on 607/607 (incl. SV shape-contract GREEN on the regenerated parser; no shape regression). RGX 44/44 (grammar-only SV change). - ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.6`
  Status: `done` `2026-05-23` — context-dependent uvm_utils failure pinned to a MINIMAL 7-line repro; root-cause hypothesis identified (fact-emission transactionality during speculative typedef parsing). Fix routed to .b.6.2.7.
  Goal: `Diagnose what makes the uvm_utils 135-line window still fail after .b.6.2.5.`
  Method: `Per-construct probing — built the failing context progressively, narrowing the trigger by progressive addition + isolation. Then --trace to find the deepest failure position.`
  Minimal repro (7 lines):
    ```
    package p;
    class C #(type TYPE=int);
      typedef TYPE T;
      function void f();
        int x = TYPE::type_name;
      endfunction
    endclass
    endpackage
    ```
    Without `typedef TYPE T;` (or replacing TYPE with a concrete type) → PASSES. Adding it back → FAILS.
  Trace evidence: `--trace shows class_scope_type's branches all rejected for 'TYPE'. known_unscoped_class_scope_class_identifier rejected (post-predicate fact_attribute_equals[type_name,TYPE,declaration_family,class] false — TYPE isn't a class). Same for interface_class branch. known_unscoped_class_scope_type_parameter_identifier also rejected (the branch that SHOULD match). So none of the class_scope alternatives work post-typedef.`
  Root-cause hypothesis: `During parsing of 'typedef TYPE T;', TYPE is consumed as a data_type/class_type for the typedef's source. Speculative parse paths through the typedef machinery emit a 'type_name{TYPE, declaration_family:typedef}' fact (or a similar mis-classification) that PERSISTS beyond the speculative context. Subsequently when 'TYPE::name' is parsed, non_typedef_package_scope's lacks_fact_attribute_equals(...,typedef) returns FALSE (TYPE now has a typedef fact) → branch rejected. AND class_scope_type's type_parameter branch may have been overwritten — the fact_attribute_equals(...,type_parameter) returns false. ALL ::-scope branches reject → TYPE::name unparseable.`
  Why this is hypothesis-strength: `(a) Probe matrix shows the failure is gated EXACTLY by typedef-of-TYPE (typedef of any other type → no effect on TYPE::name). (b) Trace shows ALL class_scope branches rejected post-typedef — consistent with TYPE's facts being mis-classified. (c) No alternate explanation accounts for the typedef-TYPE-specific trigger. CONFIRMATION via dump-facts test would lock it.`
  Sub-class: `Engine-level — fact-emission transactionality during speculative parse paths. Related to .3.3.3 IIFE transaction (which fixed ONE such leak); apparently another leak remains specifically around typedef's data_type-of-type-parameter parse path.`
  Routed to: `.3.3.4.b.6.2.7 (confirm hypothesis via dump-facts probe; fix per the no-workarounds hierarchy — likely level 4 or 5: ensure data_type/class_type parse paths emit facts only on successful commit, not during speculative attempts).`
  Commit: `pending — PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0043 (diagnosis-only)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.7`
  Status: `done` `2026-05-23` — C3 fact-leak root cause fixed at the engine level. The minimal `.b.6.2.6` 7-line repro now parses end-to-end via `parseability_probe`; lib 547/547, features-on 608/608 (one new dump-facts diagnostic test added), SV shape-contract GREEN. External-corpus triage running.
  Goal: `Confirm and fix the typedef-of-TYPE-parameter fact-emission leak diagnosed at .b.6.2.6 — TYPE acquires a spurious 'declaration_family:typedef' fact alongside its correct 'type_parameter' fact when 'typedef TYPE T;' is parsed, breaking later 'lacks_fact_attribute_equals(declaration_family,typedef)' consumers (non_typedef_package_scope, scoped_data_type_identifier) that gate 'TYPE::name'.`
  Method (per-discipline ordering — verify EBNF intent → understand root cause deeply → walk the no-workarounds fix hierarchy 1→5): `(1) Wrote a focused dump-facts test (ast_shape_contract::tests::systemverilog_b627_diag_typedef_type_parameter_fact_dump) over three controlled inputs (A: bare typedef int t; B: typedef of type_parameter TYPE; C: typedef of ordinary typedef U) to OBSERVE the fact-store state empirically — not deduce from code. (2) Result B vs C decisive: TYPE gets 'declaration_family:typedef' ×8 alongside its 'type_parameter' ×20; U/V never get a spurious extra-family — so the leak is class B-specific (the LHS-of-typedef on a name pre-existing as type_parameter). (3) Traced the codegen: declared_type_identifier IS wrapped in with_semantic_runtime_rule_transaction (the .3.3.3 IIFE fix), so the rule's OWN body is transactional — child commit on local success is correct. BUT the universal try_parse wrapper (rust/src/ast_pipeline/ast_based_generator.rs:4842, the wrapper used by EVERY PEG branch alternative, optional group ?, quantifier * / + / {N,M} iteration, AND lookahead &X/!X via generate_or_logic/generate_lookahead_logic) snapshots only self.position + self.recursion_guard.parse_stack.len() on entry; it does NOT snapshot self.semantic_runtime_state. So when SV's type_declaration_sv_2017's branch 6 ("kw_typedef ( kw_enum | kw_struct | kw_union )? declared_type_identifier semi") is speculatively tried on "typedef TYPE T;" — matches kw_typedef + the empty optional + declared_type_identifier on TYPE (which commits TYPE=typedef into the parent state via the child's per-rule transaction) — then fails on the semi token (finds " T" not ";") — try_parse rolls back position but the leaked fact persists. Later lacks_fact_attribute_equals(declaration_family,typedef) on TYPE returns false → all ::-scope branches reject → TYPE::name unparseable.`
  Fix locus (hierarchy level 5 — parser-agnostic engine; explicitly walked levels 1–4 first): `Levels 1 (existing annotations) and 2 (existing store query/predicate) can't reach this: no annotation can stop the engine from running emit_fact during a speculative branch; the store IS transactional but the engine wasn't USING the transaction at the speculative-attempt boundary. Levels 3/4 (new annotation/store primitive) also don't fit: the store ALREADY has checkpoint() / rollback_to() (.b.5.1.1 multi-index + rollback). The proper fix is at the engine layer: try_parse must snapshot the semantic state on entry and rollback_to it on failure — making the per-speculative-attempt wrapper transactional in SEMANTIC state alongside parse position. Parser-AGNOSTIC: regex / json grammars (no semantic predicates) snapshot is O(1) trivially; SV reaps the correctness fix. Change: rust/src/ast_pipeline/ast_based_generator.rs::try_parse — add saved_semantic_checkpoint = self.semantic_runtime_state.checkpoint() at entry; on Err path, self.semantic_runtime_state.rollback_to(saved_semantic_checkpoint).`
  Empirical confirmation: `Pre-fix dump (controlled three-input probe): A=t×typedef×8; B=TYPE×type_parameter×20 + TYPE×typedef×8 + T×typedef×8; C=U×typedef×16 + V×typedef×8. Post-fix dump: A=t×typedef×8 (no FAILED branch reaches declared_type_identifier on int — keyword, not non_keyword_identifier); B=TYPE×type_parameter×12 + T×typedef×8, **TYPE×typedef GONE**; C=U×typedef×8 + V×typedef×8 (U's spurious ×8 from branch-6-fail-on-semi rolled back). Exactly the predicted change. Minimal C3 repro (package p; class C #(type TYPE=int); typedef TYPE T; function void f(); int x = TYPE::type_name; endfunction endclass endpackage) → parseability_probe --parse: PASSES (was FAIL).`
  Scope LIMITATION (bug B intentionally NOT addressed here): `Under longest_match / priority_first branch policy the multi-branch codegen tries ALL branches and selects the best; loser-but-SUCCESSFUL branches' fact emissions still accumulate in the committed parent state. That's a strictly broader engine fix (commit-only-winner — either delta-replay or winner-double-execute) and is tracked separately in the taxonomy as C3-B. It is a fact-COUNT efficiency issue and does NOT corrupt boolean has_fact consumers (the SV consumer model), so it does NOT block the SV-EXH-PROOF campaign. This was verified by case A (t×typedef×8 unchanged after the fix; consumers care about presence, not count).`
  Verification: `lib 547/547 (no-features) ✓. features-on 608/608 ✓ (607 + my new dump-facts probe). SV shape-contract systemverilog_ast_shape_contract_holds_against_running_generated_parser GREEN ✓. Minimal 7-line C3 repro PARSES end-to-end via parseability_probe --parse (was FAIL). RGX integration: 18/18 green in the lib run (no separate regression). **Full external-corpus triage gate (the campaign-truth oracle) completed 2026-05-23 ~12:35 [~108 min wall, parse_total_ms=6525103 incl. uvm_pkg_2017=53min, uvm_pkg_2023=42min on debug-build]: parse_pass_total=10, parse_fail_total=4 — IDENTICAL to pre-fix baseline. Same 4 cases fail: uvm_pkg ×{2017,2023}, uvm_compat_pkg ×{2017,2023}. preprocess_pass_total=14/14 unchanged. primary_parse_failure_case=uvm_pkg (2017). All 10 previously-passing files still pass. No regression on any file.** HONEST: the C3-A fix unblocks the minimal repro and is the right engine-level fix, but the full uvm files have a deeper independent blocker exposed by C3-A's strictness — a NEW taxonomy class H1 (missing-fact-from-cross-file-import): in standalone parsing the import "uvm_pkg::*" produces no facts, so later "uvm_pkg::uvm_packer" reference fails the unscoped has_fact predicate; pre-fix this was masked by C3-A's speculative-fact leaks happening to accidentally populate the store. The accidental masking is gone; H1 surfaces cleanly. Routed to .b.6.2.8 (the new class). The deep parse position has advanced ~1000 lines (5521 → 6591) per the safe-cut bisection — this real forward motion is invisible at the corpus-pass-count surface (a tooling artifact tracked as F1 in the taxonomy).`
  Commit: `d26fc139 — PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0044`

  Status: `pending` — needs debug-info rebuild + symbol-rich profiling to localize the catastrophic-backtrack hot rule.
  Goal: `Localize the exact rule causing the catastrophic-backtrack loop at byte ~118186 (line 4029 of uvm_pkg in task uvm_hdl_force_time's body) that hangs the parse when line 11755 is also present.`

  Outcome (LANDED 2026-05-24 — MAJOR ENGINE FIX): `Diagnosis via codegen-cross-analysis: memoization (Packrat) was applied ONLY to recursive rules in ast_based_generator.rs::generate_rule_function (gating: if is_recursive). Non-recursive leaf rules (identifier, simple_identifier, number, string_literal, primary_literal, etc.) were never memoized — every visit at the same byte position was a fresh computation. Trace analysis pinned this: identifier visited 96× at byte 117064, package_scope 56× at 118186, data_type 15× at multiple positions. FIX (level 5 parser-agnostic engine): apply memoized_call to ALL rules unconditionally — true Packrat O(N) semantics. Memory cost: HashMap entry per (rule_id, position) actually queried (O(N log N) typical, not N×R worst-case). Acceptable trade. Empirical: head -11755 + endpkg: pre-fix >180s TIMEOUT → post-fix 22s PASS. Test gates all green (547 + 608 + SV shape). Corpus 10/14 stable. uvm_pkg safe-cut bisection: last-PASS line 11754 → 19378 (+7624 lines). CUMULATIVE pre-fix → now: 5521 → 19378 = +13857 lines (~15% of 90K file) — LARGEST single-fix advance of the campaign.`
  Commit: `71470852 — PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0061`
