- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.5`
  Status: `done` (2026-06-10)
  Goal: `Two inventory-wide auto-gates fail on the regex + rtl_const_expr parsers (auto_gate_regex_inventory_wide_shape, auto_gate_rtl_const_expr_inventory_wide_shape) with: 'rule subroutine_call/1: declared key target missing from parsed value: {body:[...],kind:capturing_group,type:atom}' on input '(a|b)*c'. Decisively verified PRE-EXISTING at .3.3.2 (75afb3c7) via git-stash baseline check — they fail at the committed state with no SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.3 changes applied. The RGX broader corpus/conformance gate (44/0) and SV shape-contract are GREEN, so this is a baseline inventory-vs-emission drift (the auto-gate's discriminator matches a node whose emitted shape lost the declared 'target' key), NOT a parsing regression. Requires FULL root-cause + fix (not just acknowledged): trace which inventory entry declares 'target', why subroutine_call/1's emitted shape doesn't have it, whether the inventory or the emission needs correction, then leaf-owned grammar/inventory fix + same-commit lockstep.`
  Acceptance: `auto_gate_regex_inventory_wide_shape + auto_gate_rtl_const_expr_inventory_wide_shape both PASS; no other-test regression; root cause documented; leaf-owned + lockstep.`
  Verification: `done — ROOT CAUSE (tools-first; answers the leaf's question with a third option): NEITHER the
  inventory NOR the emission is wrong — the GATE'S MATCHER was. run_inventory_wide_auto_gate keyed its
  discriminator map on the type: literal ALONE (HashMap<String, descriptor>), but many entries
  legitimately share one type literal and are distinguished by a FURTHER literal: regex has 32
  type:"atom" entries split by kind:, rtl_const_expr has 10 type:"binop_chain" entries split by level:.
  HashMap::insert let the LAST-inserted same-type entry win — the inventory is alphabetical-by-rule
  (feedback_manifest_alphabetical_order), so regex's winner was subroutine_call/1 (declares target) and
  rtl_const_expr's was shift_expr/0 (declares level:"shift"): every type:"atom" node (e.g. the healthy
  {type:atom, kind:capturing_group, body} from '(a|b)*c') was verified against subroutine_call's keys →
  the false 'target missing' failure; every binop_chain node against level:"shift" → the false level
  mismatches. The gate was written when regex had only 2 typed-Object entries with UNIQUE type literals
  (per the test header comment); the typing campaigns broke the uniqueness assumption silently. FIX
  (gate-oracle only, parser-agnostic, auto_return_annotation_shape_gate.rs): the map now groups ALL
  same-type descriptors (HashMap<String, Vec<_>>) and the walker matches a runtime object against each
  candidate's FULL string-literal tuple (descriptor_literals_match: every declared literal key/value
  carried verbatim); pass iff ANY tuple-matching candidate verifies (distinct branches may declare the
  same tuple, e.g. subroutine_call/0+/1); a node whose tuple matches NO same-type entry is now an
  EXPLICIT drift failure (detection sharpened, not weakened); coverage reporting (discriminators
  declared/seen/not-covered) intentionally unchanged (still type-literal-keyed). VERIFIED: both
  auto-gates PASS; ALL 9 grammar integration auto-gates PASS (was 7/2); new module unit test
  inventory_wide_gate_distinguishes_same_type_entries_by_full_literal_tuple locks the collision class
  (healthy tuple-match passes / missing-key fails against the RIGHT entry / unmatched tuple = drift);
  module tests 16/0; FULL WORKSPACE cargo test --features generated_parsers 759/0 (first fully-green
  full-workspace run — composes with SV-EXH-PROOF.8's E0063 fix) + dual-feature
  "generated_parsers ebnf_dual_run" 788/0; clippy strict-source clean. NO grammar/generated/inventory/
  parser change (test-oracle fix only); the gate's eprintln coverage warnings (regex 7 type-literals +
  rtl_const_expr ternary/unary not exercised by the curated samples) remain visible as future
  sample-coverage improvements, deliberately NOT folded into this slice.`
  Commit: `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0155`

  Status: `done`
  Goal: `Investigate why target_timeout_errors_total (469) exceeds primary_entry_attempts_total (432) in the aggregate-contract gate's shadow-probe report. Likely root cause: timeouts during PROBE attempts (sub-rule discovery) are counted as target_timeouts but are NOT counted as primary entry attempts; the gate's invariant assumes a 1:1 mapping that doesn't hold.   Verification: `done — WHY+WHERE pinned tools-first. WHERE: primary_entry_attempts_total is a MISNOMER — main.rs:566 maps it to TargetDriveValidationSummary.validated_outputs (primary generations that SUCCEEDED, incremented only on the Ok branch at stimuli_generator.rs:2270-2271); target_timeout_errors_total counts primary generations that TIMED OUT (the Err branch at 2329-2331). WHY: success and timeout are mutually-exclusive per-attempt outcomes (disjoint buckets), so on a hard-to-generate corpus timeouts legitimately exceed validated outputs (observed: 432 succeeded + 469 timed out = ~901 primary attempts). 469 > 432 is COHERENT DATA, not corruption. Same applies to the helper sibling (alternate_entry_attempts = alt successes @2259 vs helper_timeout_errors = alt timeouts @2326). - ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.5.2.3`
  Status: `done`
  Goal: `Fix the aggregate-contract gate's reachable-branch universe drift check (rust/scripts/sv_parser_aggregate_contract_gate.sh:312 — "replay_reachable_branches != initial_reachable_branches"). It assumes reachable_branches is a STATIC grammar property like reachable_rules, but it is a DYNAMIC remaining-coverage-gap quantity: stimuli_generator.rs computes it with "if deficit == 0 { continue }" at line 1693, so branches already covered to threshold are EXCLUDED from reachable_branches (and absorbed into unreachable_branches via total - reachable at line 1837). The closed-loop replay's whole PURPOSE is to cover branches, which shrinks reachable_branches (observed 1409->890 fresh, 1409->483 cached). The check therefore fails on any productive replay. ASYMMETRIC from the sibling reachable_rules drift check (line 307), which IS valid because reachable_rules = compute_reachable_rules() static graph traversal (line 1562) and stayed 1271==1271. Fix: replace the != equality drift check on reachable_branches with the structurally-correct monotonic check (replay_reachable_branches <= initial_reachable_branches — the gap may shrink but must not grow), OR drop it in favour of the already-present covered_reachable_branches non-regression check (line 297). Keep the reachable_rules drift check intact.`
  Acceptance: `Aggregate-contract gate passes end-to-end (reaches summary stage, exit 0); family-status gate advances past the aggregate sub-gate; reachable_rules drift check (line 307) preserved; WHY+WHERE documented tools-first per [[feedback_why_and_where_before_solution]].`
  Verification: `done — chose the monotonic-debt fix: replaced "replay_reachable_branches != initial_reachable_branches" with "replay_reachable_branches > initial_reachable_branches" (error message reworded to "remaining reachable-branch gap grew across focused replay"), mirroring the existing replay_target_count>initial_target_count debt idiom at line 287. Kept the reachable_rules != equality check (line 307) intact (it IS a static universe). Added a durable explanatory comment. VERIFIED: (a) standalone aggregate-contract gate EXISTING-mode against the .5.2.2 fresh matched artifacts (initial=1409 replay=890) reaches the summary stage and exits 0 — "✅ SV parser aggregate contract gate passed."; (b) full fresh family-status gate run: sv_parser_aggregate_contract_gate sub-gate = ok (advanced past it), AND sv_syntax_closure + sv_preprocessor_syntax_closure + sv_preprocessor_aggregate_contract + sv_preprocessor_reachability_closure + sv_preprocessor_formal_exhaustive_closure all = ok. Family-status now fails on a NEW sub-gate (sv_semantic_scope_contract_gate: case parameter_typedef_fail profile 2023 expected=fail actual=pass) — routed to .5.2.4. Gate-script only, no grammar/Rust/generated change, no release bump.`
  Commit: `done — SVEXH-Slice-94 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0105)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.5.2.4`
  Status: `done` (tools-first INVESTIGATION — pure docs; fix routed to `.5.2.4.1`)
  Goal: `sv_semantic_scope_contract_gate fails: case parameter_typedef_fail (profile 2023, the suite's only mismatch, 1/20) is "module m; typedef int T; localparam int Y = T::P; endmodule" with expect_pass:false, but the parser now ACCEPTS it. Pin WHY (which rule/branch accepts T::P) + WHERE (exact grammar rule) with the AST-dump/trace tooling BEFORE proposing a fix; document fix candidates + regression risk.`
  Acceptance: `Complete WHY+WHERE pinned with tools; fix design candidates + uvm-regression risk documented; fix routed to its own leaf (.5.2.4.1) for empirical regen+corpus iteration. (No grammar change in this leaf — investigation only.)`
  Verification: `done — REPRODUCED: parseability_probe --parse systemverilog (T::P case) profile 2023 = "parse_full passed" (should reject). WHERE (via --parse-dump-ast-pretty): the localparam value T::P parses as {kind: data_type, body: {kind: provisional_class_type, head: T, scope_chain: [::P]}}. PATH: local_parameter_declaration "typed" branch (kw_localparam data_type_or_implicit list_of_param_assignments) -> param_assignment value $4 = constant_param_expression -> its "data_type" branch (grammars/systemverilog.ebnf:1308, valid per IEEE 1800 §A.2.4 constant_param_expression ::= ... | data_type) -> data_type branch provisional_unscoped_block_class_type (line 1714). WHY: provisional_unscoped_block_class_type (line 1654-1655) is gated ONLY by has_fact(type_name, $head.body); "typedef int T" DOES emit a type_name fact for T, so T passes — even though the scope_chain (::P) requires T to be a class/package. Its sibling known_unscoped_block_class_type (line 1649-1651) has the CORRECT extra gate lacks_fact_attribute_equals(type_name, $head, declaration_family, typedef) and would reject T. The other six *_typedef_fail cases (tf_call/type_cast/property/sequence/let/checker) reject correctly because their paths do NOT route through provisional_unscoped_block_class_type. CONFIRMED bare "T x;" does NOT use this rule (routes via net_declaration/known_unscoped_data_type paths) — so the unscoped typedef-as-datatype case is unaffected by tightening. FIX CANDIDATES (deferred to .5.2.4.1 for empirical iteration): (a) add lacks_fact_attribute_equals(...declaration_family, typedef) to provisional_unscoped_block_class_type (matches its known_ sibling) — risk: may over-reject if uvm needs a typedef head; (b) split provisional into unscoped (permissive) + scoped (requires class/package head) so only "Head::member" is gated — preserves the bare-typedef-as-class-type case; (c) swap data_type's branch (line 1714) from provisional to known_unscoped_block_class_type. REGRESSION RISK: uvm_pkg uses scope-resolution heavily; the fix MUST keep SV external corpus 14/14 — needs regen + full corpus triage per candidate.`
  Commit: `done — SVEXH-Slice-95 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0106), pure docs`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.1`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.2`
  Status: `done` (design recorded + ACCEPTED 2026-06-01; code split to .7.2.1+)
  Goal: `DESIGN (pure docs) the parser-agnostic deterministic target-reach / path-forcing mechanism that drives focused_replay_target_count -> 0.`
  Acceptance: `design recorded (mechanism + where it hooks into stimuli_generator.rs + how it stays GENERAL + verification plan); implementation is a SEPARATE code-leaf (.7.2.1+) opened only after the design is accepted.`

Mechanism = a TargetReachPlan generalizing two existing engine primitives (the mutation_active all-candidates bypass @~3636 + forced_or_branch_for_site per-site forcing @~3699), driven by StimuliCoverageTarget.depends_on (@515) + a predecessor-tracked compute_reachable_rules (@2927); GENERAL (no hardcoded rule names) per [[feedback_ast_pipeline_parser_agnostic]]; NO-WORKAROUNDS level 5 (minimal engine generalization, not a kludge); decomposed into .7.2.1..7.2.4 with per-target + aggregate + hard no-regression verification (corpus 14/14, lib 609/609, RGX 44/0). ACCEPTED by director 2026-06-01 → .7.2.1 code campaign authorized. The .6 clean-run prerequisite is satisfied (-0113).`
  Commit: `done — SVEXH-Slice-102 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0113)`

  ### .7.2 CAMPAIGN SUMMARY (consolidated state + observations + conclusions — 2026-06-02, HEAD 5aa3d623)

  **Goal:** carefully amend `rust/src/ast_pipeline/stimuli_generator.rs`, in a
  GENERAL parser-agnostic way (no hardcoded rule names/sigils, benefits every PGEN
  parser per [[feedback_ast_pipeline_parser_agnostic]]), to cover every reachable
  rule+branch of the grammar → drive `focused_replay_target_count` to literal 0.

  **What was built (the reach machinery; all landed + tested, all parser-agnostic):**
  - `.7.2.1` `compute_reach_path` + `collect_rule_reference_sites` — reach-path graph analysis (no generation change).
  - `.7.2.2` `ActiveReachPlan` wired into the OR-decision (steer the forced branch); default-None → byte-identical production path until installed.
  - `.7.2.3` `ReachOutcome` (Reached/SelectedButFailed/NotReached) — honest per-plan outcome from existing coverage counters.
  - `.7.2.4`/`.7.2.5` driver hook + `reach_plan_activations` observability.
  - `.7.2.7` fixed the `.7.2.4` dead-guard (deeper-fallback threshold) → hook actually fires on the corpus.
  - `.7.2.16` read-only `reach_classification` in the gap report (route B).
  - `.7.2.19` `compute_rule_reach_target` — rule→introducing-branch translation (analysis only).

  **Measured results (full `sv_parser_aggregate_contract_gate` closed-loop replay; replay_target_count = residual, lower is better; deterministic seed so signal-not-noise):**
  | state | replay_target_count | resolved/2660 | gen_successes | note |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | `.7.2.6` dead hook (activations=0) | 1273 | 1387 | 714 | hook never fired |
  | **`.7.2.8` baseline (concentrated steering)** | **888** | **1772** | **2221** | BEST-KNOWN; the kept state |
  | `.7.2.10` quantifier-forcing only | 1982 | 679 | 4615 | REGRESSION (reverted .7.2.13) |
  | `.7.2.12` quantifier-forcing + rotation | 1717 | 943 | 2359 | REGRESSION (reverted) |
  | `.7.2.20` rule-reach steering | 1883 | 777 | 4840 | REGRESSION (discarded uncommitted) |

  **Key observation (the durable finding):** every steering-INTENSIFICATION attempt
  REGRESSED via the SAME mechanism — **diversity collapse**: forcing the generator
  toward specific targets raises generation_successes (more valid samples) but those
  samples are near-IDENTICAL, so resolved coverage FALLS. The ablation (`.7.2.14`)
  isolated `.7.2.10`-only as worst (1982); rule-reach (`.7.2.20`) reproduced it
  (1883). So the lever is wrong: **aggressive target-steering of any kind hurts**;
  the gentle/concentrated `.7.2.8` steering at **888** is this approach's CEILING.

  **Reachability evidence (`.7.2.17`, the signoff fact):** the residual is **100%
  reachable** — partition (1717 sample): 967 reachable_by_plan + 750
  reachable_rule_not_generated + **0 no_reach_path**. So literal-0 is attainable in
  PRINCIPLE; the gap is generator capability, NOT grammar structure. (888 baseline =
  same classes, fewer of each; not separately re-measured — route-invariant.)

  **Conclusion:** the target-steering family of amendments has been honestly
  exhausted (3 measured regressions). 888 is the best the steering approach reaches.
  Reaching literal-0 needs a DIFFERENT parser-agnostic mechanism — one that
  PRESERVES sample diversity while reaching rare targets (not more forcing). That is
  `.7.2.21`'s decision: accept-with-evidence at 888 (literal-0 = tracked future
  enhancement requiring a new mechanism) vs commission that new mechanism now —
  DIRECTOR call (literal-0 was the director-set bar). AWAITING that decision.

  **Invariants held throughout:** every code change task-tree-owned first; every
  regression measured-before-commit (uncommitted) or reverted byte-identical to the
  proven-good 888 blob; `origin/main` never polluted (still at the pre-campaign
  state); generation path currently byte-identical to 888 (HEAD = `.7.2.19`, only
  read-only analysis/reporting added on top). Discipline references:
  [[feedback_no_codebase_change_without_tool_backed_facts]] (born from this
  campaign's `.7.2.10` regression), [[feedback_ast_pipeline_parser_agnostic]].

NO-WORKAROUNDS level 1-2 (reuses existing coverage infrastructure; level 3+ unnecessary — the honest outcome is already fully determined by the selected/success counters the engine records). - ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.2.6`
  Status: `done` (`-0120`, 2026-06-01) — measurement complete; .7.2.4 flaw EMPIRICALLY confirmed; fix routed to .7.2.7
  Goal: `Run sv_parser_aggregate_contract_gate to completion (closed-loop replay carrying the .7.2.4 hook + the .7.2.5 reach_plan_activations readout) and record: (a) reach_plan_activations (did the hook fire on the real SV corpus?), (b) replay_target_count vs the ≈1482/~2660-initial baseline, (c) corpus 14/14 unchanged. The gate is slow (~50min for the 5000-attempt replay) so this is a dedicated run.`
  Acceptance: `reach_plan_activations recorded from a completed gate run; replay_target_count before/after quantified; if the hook fired but the count did not drop, investigate whether the never_selected residual is genuinely unsatisfiable (classify reach_target_outcome per residual target) → director decision on Done-with-residual vs further .7.2.x tuning; corpus 14/14; gate still passes (or its failure is an honest pre-existing one, not a .7.2 regression).`
  Verification: `done — full sv_parser_aggregate_contract_gate run bfotrx6jg completed the 5000-attempt closed-loop replay. AUTHORITATIVE READOUT (profile_2017 closed-loop replay summary line): "resolved 1387/2660 targets in 5000 attempts (generation_successes=714, generation_errors=4286, target_timeout_errors=784, helper_timeout_errors=3502, reach_plan_activations=0)". So: (1) reach_plan_activations=0 CONFIRMS EMPIRICALLY the .7.2.4 hook NEVER FIRED on the real SV corpus — the design flaw is real, not just theorized. ROOT CAUSE: the hook guard "!helper_probe_active && stagnant>=probe_threshold" is mutually exclusive with the loop's own behavior — generation_entry is switched to a HELPER-PROBE rule exactly when stagnant>=probe_threshold (line 2523), so helper_probe_active is always true when the stagnation guard is met. (2) BASELINE replay_target_count=1273 (down from initial_gap=2660; resolved 1387). Residual reasons: 591 never_selected + 464 never_hit + 218 selected_but_failed. Residual types: 809 branch + 464 rule. The 591 never_selected branch targets are exactly the reach-driver's intended quarry. - ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.2.7`
  Status: `done` (`-0121`, 2026-06-01) — dead-guard FIXED; hook fires (unit-proven); corpus re-measurement routed to .7.2.8
  Goal: `Make reach-steering ENGAGE on stagnation. The .7.2.4/.7.2.6 flaw: the hook guard !helper_probe_active && stagnant>=probe_threshold could never be true (the loop switches generation_entry to a helper-probe rule exactly when stagnant>=probe_threshold → helper_probe_active=true), measured activations=0.`
  Acceptance: `reach_plan_activations>0 demonstrably (hook fires); no non-termination; lib + clippy green; SV corpus structurally unaffected (generator-only). Corpus replay_target_count delta vs the 1273 baseline is the .7.2.8 measurement.`

DESIGN ITERATION (recorded honestly): first cut preempted on ANY pending Branch at probe_threshold → broke target_driven_generation_filter_keeps_alternate_probe_helper_coverage (starved alternate-entry probing for helper Rule targets); narrowing to pending[0]==Branch still broke it (that test's top target IS the branch); the deeper-fallback threshold is the principled fix — helper-probe gets its window first, reach-steering only engages once stagnation PERSISTS past it (the real corpus hit stagnation=139 with probe_threshold=8 → reach_threshold=24, fires comfortably). VERIFIED: NEW test reach_hook_fires_under_deep_stagnation builds a selected-but-fails branch target that drives probe_threshold→8 + stagnation past 24 and asserts reach_plan_activations>=1 (regression guard for the dead-guard flaw — this is the empirical proof the hook FIRES, replacing the .7.2.4 activations=0). lib no-features 561/561 (+1); lib --features generated_parsers 622/622 (+1); the previously-broken helper-coverage test PASSES again; source clippy clean. NO grammar/codegen/generated change (generator runtime-only), no release bump. NO-WORKAROUNDS level 5 (parser-agnostic generator capability). The real-corpus replay_target_count re-measurement (does the now-firing hook push below 1273 toward literal-0 per director) needs the slow aggregate gate → .7.2.8.`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.2.9`
  Status: `done` (`-0123`, 2026-06-01, pure-docs INVESTIGATION) — root-caused the 888 residual; two reinforcing fixes routed to .7.2.10 (quantifier-force) + .7.2.11 (head-of-line rotation)
  Goal: `Tools-first: classify the 888 residual + pin WHY 417 never_selected branches resist after the hook fires 2606× but only +385 net resolved (~15% conversion).`
  Acceptance: `each residual class explained mechanistically with grammar/source evidence before any code change.`
  Verification: `done — dissected profile_2017_replay_gap.json (888 targets). Residual = 417 never_selected branch + 236 never_hit rule + 235 selected_but_failed branch. Two reinforcing ROOT CAUSES pinned (tools-first, no guessing):
   (1) QUANTIFIER GAP on the reach path. directives_along_path (stimuli_generator.rs) emits a ReachDirective only for OR (o{i}) segments; it IGNORES quantifier (q) segments. And generate_quantified has no reach-plan awareness — for a `*`/`?` it can pick repeats=0 (select_preferred_quantifier_repeat) and skip the subtree. CONFIRMED via grammar: always_keyword (the perpetual pending[0], branch #2 = kw_always_latch, a trivially-forceable keyword) is reached only through hop-path bodies quantified with `*` (module_item* / statement_or_null* / non_port_module_item* etc., grammar lines 806/823/2155...). So even a correct OR-forcing plan is skipped when an on-path quantifier rolls 0 → the target rule is never entered. Only 20/417 have `/q` in their OWN target path, but the HOP paths between entry and target are full of `*`/`+` → this is the dominant 'never reached' cause. FIX = .7.2.10: extend the reach plan to force on-path quantifiers to >=max(min_repeat,1), and make generate_quantified honor it (mirror the existing forced_quantifier_repeats_for_site mechanism).
   (2) HEAD-OF-LINE BLOCKING in the driver. The .7.2.7 hook installs a plan for pending[0] EVERY stagnation window; pending is sorted (priority desc, then id asc) so the SAME top target (branch::always_keyword::root#2, prio 1464, lowest id) is retried every window. If it can't be resolved (because of cause 1), pending[0] never rotates → the 2606 activations were largely spent re-hammering a handful of head-of-line targets, never advancing to the other ~416 (explains the ~15% conversion). FIX = .7.2.11: rotate/round-robin across pending branch targets (skip targets a plan already failed to advance this run; e.g. cycle by attempt index or track a per-run 'recently-steered' set) so activations spread across the residual.
   Both are GENERAL/parser-agnostic generator-level changes (no rule-name special-casing). Pure docs/measurement here — NO code change.`
  Commit: `done — SVEXH-Slice-112 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0123)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.2.10`
  Status: `done` (`-0124`, 2026-06-01) — on-path quantifier forcing landed; verified by unit test
  Goal: `Extend the reach plan to FORCE on-path quantifiers so the target subtree is entered. (a) compute_reach_path / directives_along_path also records each q-site crossed on the hop+target paths as a quantifier-force directive (force repeats >= max(min_repeat,1)); (b) generate_quantified consults the active reach plan (like it already consults forced_quantifier_repeats_for_site) and, when the current (rule,node_path) is an on-path q-site, prefers a >=1 repeat candidate. Bounded by the existing depth/fuel guards so termination holds.`
  Acceptance: `a unit test where a target nested under a `*` on the reach path is now REACHED (selected_counts 0->>=1) whereas before the quantifier could skip it; the reach_hook_fires + steering tests still pass; lib + clippy green; generator-only; corpus structurally unaffected. (Corpus replay_target_count re-measure happens after .7.2.11, in .7.2.12.)`
  Verification: `done — implemented. compute_reach_path refactored to a new compute_reach_plan that returns (Vec<ReachDirective>, HashSet<(rule,node_path)> quantifier_sites); collect_quantifier_sites_along_path walks each hop's raw reference-site path AND the target rule's path-to-OR-node, recording every `q` segment crossed as (rule, prefix-up-to-q). ActiveReachPlan gained quantifier_sites + is_on_path_quantifier(); from_directives takes the set; set_reach_plan threads it. generate_quantified now, when a plan marks THIS (current_rule,node_path) as an on-path quantifier site, forces repeats >= max(min_repeat,1) clamped to [min,bounded_max], trying the forced count FIRST then remaining counts highest-first as fallbacks (termination preserved). compute_reach_path kept as a thin wrapper (drops the q-set) so the .7.2.1 tests are unchanged. VERIFIED: NEW test reach_plan_forces_on_path_quantifier_to_enter_target — a target under `start := pre body*` (deep::root#2 inside the `*`) is now reached (the `*` site start::root/s1 is recorded + forced; selected_counts 0->>=1; output contains 'r'); all 12 reach + 2 driver tests still pass. lib no-features 562/562 (+1); lib --features generated_parsers 623/623 (+1); source clippy clean. NO grammar/codegen/generated change (generator runtime-only), no release bump. NO-WORKAROUNDS level 5 (parser-agnostic generator capability; keyed only on (rule,node_path)). Corpus replay_target_count re-measure deferred to .7.2.12 (after .7.2.11).`
  Commit: `done — SVEXH-Slice-113 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0124)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.2.12`
  Status: `done` (`-0126`, 2026-06-01) — MEASURED a REGRESSION: .7.2.10+.7.2.11 made it WORSE (888 → 1717); revert/gate routed to .7.2.13
  Goal: `Full aggregate-gate run; record reach_plan_activations + replay_target_count vs the .7.2.8 baseline of 888.`

ROOT CAUSE of the regression (data-confirmed): DIVERSITY COLLAPSE. (a) .7.2.10's forced on-path quantifier expansion (>=1, highest-first) makes generation produce DEEPER/LONGER samples that time out far more — target_timeout_errors 784 → 1837 (2.3×). (b) .7.2.11's rotation spreads steering thin across many targets, each sample covering its one narrow target but little incidentally. generation_successes actually rose (2221 → 2359) while resolved FELL (1772 → 943) — the tell of narrow, low-incidental-coverage samples crowding out the broad coverage .7.2.8's concentrated steering produced for free. The fixes are individually unit-correct (they DO reach quantifier-nested targets + rotate) but globally counterproductive on the closed-loop replay. CONTAINMENT: regression is in UNPUSHED commits only — origin/main is still at .7.2.8 (c6480943, the good 888 state). VERIFIED via git. Per director (literal-0): 1717 != 0 and worse, so this path is REJECTED. → .7.2.13 = revert to .7.2.8 behavior (gate the .7.2.10+.7.2.11 aggressiveness OFF by default; keep the tested mechanism behind a flag) OR git-revert the two slices; re-measure to confirm return to ~888; then rethink the approach (the concentrated .7.2.7 steering beat both ablations — the real lever may be smarter target SELECTION, not more forcing). NO code change in this measurement leaf.`
  Commit: `done — SVEXH-Slice-115 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0126)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.3`
  Status: `done` (`-0137`, 2026-06-02, pure-docs RESEARCH) — literature-grounded proof that literal-0 IS systematically attainable; supersedes the accept-at-888 framing
  Goal: `Per director directive 2026-06-02 ("reaching Literal-0 needs real research … demonstrate it is possible to systematically, elegantly amend the stimuli generator to reach Literal-0 … research academia/papers/books … stop randomly trying things then revert"): survey the published state of the art for grammar-based test generation / grammar coverage and produce a tangible, citation-backed design that demonstrates literal-0 is attainable (or proves it is not).`
  Verification: `done — 3 parallel literature agents (~120 web searches/fetches, primary-source citations, verbatim-flagged). Synthesis written to docs/tasks/SV-EXH-PROOF-7.3-literature-grounded-literal-zero-design.md. KEY FINDINGS: (1) our problem = "grammar-based test generation" under a "grammar coverage" criterion (RC/CDRC/k-path/CCC taxonomy); (2) our 3 steering regressions are textbook MODE COLLAPSE / over-exploitation — RLCheck (ICSE 2020) documents the exact failure (target-hit reward w/o novelty term → one template), AFLGo (CCS 2017) + seed-scheduling avoid it by phase-separating exploration/exploitation and ADDING energy to targets, never reshaping the population; DGL (Maurer 1990) = cautionary weights-give-no-guarantee ancestor; (3) CONVERGENT ANSWER across all 3 surveys = DECOUPLE: diverse background via unbiased/count-uniform/Boltzmann sampling (Flajolet-Zimmermann-Van Cutsem 1994; Duchon et al. 2004) + per-residual MINIMAL WITNESS via Purdom shortest-derivation (BIT 1972), appended as a SEPARATE segment, never reshaping the shared pass; (4) LITERAL-0 PROOF: Purdom guarantees a finite short-sentence set hitting every production; Hennessy & Power (ASE 2005/EMSE 2008) empirically confirm 100% rule coverage on ISO C++ is reached ONLY by constructively ADDING the last witnesses (set-cover completion), NOT by sampling more — so one minimal witness per residual obligation ⇒ residual=0, diversity preserved because witnesses are only added; (5) Layer 3 = our existing forced_or_branch_for_site / compute_reach_path is the PEG-forcing layer (matches [[project_sv7_never_selected_rootcause]] "reachability not weighting"); (6) directly-implementable form = Fuzzing Book GrammarCoverageFuzzer two-tier "uncovered-first then uniform-random". Bibliography: 20+ primary sources. NO code change.`
  Commit: `SVEXH-Slice-126 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0137)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.4.3a`
  Status: `done` (`-0140`, 2026-06-02, pure-docs ROOT-CAUSE INVESTIGATION — tools-first, head-on)
  Goal: `Per director directive ("fully explain step-by-step WHY the fail before any fix; use the toolbox; debug head-on"): establish the complete, source+data-backed causal chain for why the residual targets are never covered.`
  Verification: `done — docs/tasks/SV-EXH-PROOF-7.4.3a-residual-failure-rootcause.md. FACTS (toolbox): (F1) residual = never_hit rules + never_selected + selected_but_failed branches, all reach-classified reachable; (F2) covered_reachable_branches=0 is a DEAD-COUNTER artifact (stimuli_generator.rs:1849 continue makes :1886 unreachable), NOT a bug — branch coverage IS recorded via covered_branches; (F3) the rules generate FINE standalone (always_keyword → all 4 branches; ansi_port_declaration → 30/30 at depth 24) so they are NOT ungeneratable; (F4 decisive) DEPTH SENSITIVITY — ansi_port_declaration FAILS at max_depth ≤9, succeeds 20/20 at ≥10 (min generation-recursion depth = 10); property_expr_sv_2017 deeper still + pathologically slow; (F5 WHERE) depth limit enforced at :4377 ("depth exceeded max_depth"), near-limit OR-decision forced to terminating branch at :4509/:5413 (recursion guard, tested :9847), existing depth-slack retry at :4869. CAUSAL CHAIN: SV grammar is deeply factored → descending from systemverilog_file consumes the 24-level budget → reaching a deep rule leaves < its minimum subtree depth → :4377 errors → sample fails → selected_but_failed/never_hit; deeper branches hit the near-limit termination guard → never_selected. ROOT CAUSE = DEPTH BUDGET, not weighting/steering/counting. Explains why .7.2.x steering regressed: forcing the path to a deep target leaves its subtree to generate within the same exhausted budget → selected_but_failed (no gain) + shared-budget cannibalization → diversity collapse. NO code change.`
  Commit: `SVEXH-Slice-129 (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0140)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.4.3`

Addresses the actual cause (depth exhaustion) that plain reach-steering could not.`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.4.4`
  Status: `done` (code — PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0143, 2026-06-03; Purdom witness ordering landed + measured)
  Goal: `RE-SCOPED 2026-06-03 by .7.4.4.1's tools-first evidence. The original hypothesis (the "other" tail = CONTEXT-GATED rules whose @predicate needs ancestor store facts) is DISPROVEN: store-gated rules generate fine standalone, and with a reason-split classifier the genuine-error count is ZERO across 30- and 150-target real-SV samples (other=0; the failures are target_timeout = slow witnesses). So the residual tail is a GENERATION-PERFORMANCE problem: the deeply-factored SV rules (bins_or_options, interface_declaration_sv_2017, ansi_port_declaration, ...) take a long time to generate a witness because the generator explores deep factored alternatives. FIX direction (literature-grounded, parser-agnostic): make the slow witnesses converge fast via PURDOM min-length-guided greedy expansion — reuse .7.4.2's compute_min_terminal_lengths table to, at each choice during a witness generation, prefer the shortest-terminating subtree (Purdom 1972 shortest-derivation), so the witness completes quickly instead of timing out. Secondary lever: a tuned per-witness budget. Measure replay_target_count vs 888; MONOTONE (additive).`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.4.6`
  Status: `in progress` (RE-SCOPED by profiling — see Verification; `.7.4.6.1` done, `.7.4.6.2` is the big win)
  Goal: `The .7.4.5 residual (273) is still ~91% target_timeout: witnesses generated BY SEARCH (random + Purdom ORDERING + timeout) still exhaust the budget on the deepest rules, and budget is a diminishing lever (.7.4.5 curve). OUT-OF-THE-BOX, literature-grounded direction (Purdom 1972 shortest-derivation, taken to its conclusion): replace witness SEARCH with witness CONSTRUCTION — directly build the minimal derivation tree to each target by always expanding the shortest-terminating production (the .7.4.2 compute_min_terminal_lengths table already gives the per-node minimum), top-down, BACKTRACK-FREE. A constructed witness completes in O(tree-size) with NO timeout, dissolving the budget-vs-coverage tradeoff and driving 273 -> literal-0 (or an honest irreducible remainder surfaced with cause). Parser-agnostic; monotone (witness-pass-only). Needs a design slice first (where construction plugs into generate_target_witnesses / a new construct path), then director sign-off before the generation change lands.`
  Acceptance: `design slice (tools-first) pinning the construction path; then implementation: 273 -> 0 (or honest remainder); MONOTONE; measured via the canonical gate; director sign-off before landing.`
  Verification: `RE-SCOPED by profiling (director signed off the generation change via "(a)"). WHY+WHERE-first: a macOS sample profile of slow witness generation (interface_declaration_sv_2017, 40 witnesses, 473s) shows the witness slowness is NOT search/backtracking (so a derivation-CONSTRUCTION engine is NOT needed) — it is REDUNDANT RECOMPUTATION: (1) node_is_nullable (~26K self-time samples) recomputes a static grammar property recursively per generate_sequence call (no cache; recurses through referenced rules); (2) regex compilation (~6K) recompiles terminal patterns per generation. So the fix is surgical CACHING, monotone, no regen (generation uses the grammar, not generated parsers). Sub-leaves: .7.4.6.1 DONE; .7.4.6.2 = the big win.`
  Commit: `(re-scoped; sub-leaves below)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.4.6.2`
  Status: `done` (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0147, 2026-06-04) — landed + verified; HONEST: a real speedup, but NOT enough for literal-0 (see below)
  Goal: `Cache node_is_nullable (the dominant ~26K-sample witness cost; recomputes a STATIC property recursively per call).`
  Verification: `done — implemented a SOUND, monotone-BY-CONSTRUCTION cache (avoids the fixpoint-equivalence risk): thread-local NULLABLE_CACHE, grammar-guarded (cleared in new() when the active grammar changes; rule-name keys are grammar-specific). A rule's nullability is cached ONLY when its computation was CYCLE-FREE (node_is_nullable now threads a hit_cycle flag; a back-edge into visiting or the depth cutoff sets it) — i.e. context-INDEPENDENT, so a cache hit equals the per-call DFS exactly. VERIFIED MONOTONE: lib (no-features) 588/588 (output unchanged), source-strict clippy 0, same-seed determinism byte-identical. SPEEDUP MEASURED: interface_declaration_sv_2017 x10 90.5s vs ~118s baseline = ~24% (matches the profiled ~25% addressable share; combined with .6.1 regex cache ~25-40%). KEEP: a free monotone speedup for ALL generation (witness + diverse pass + gate runtime). ⚠️ HONEST FINDING (corrects the .7.4.6 "caching not construction" re-scope): 9s/witness is still ~45x the gate's 200ms budget, so caching does NOT bridge seconds->200ms and does NOT meaningfully reduce the literal-0 residual (273). The witnesses are fundamentally slow from the SEARCH itself (deep backtracking, even Purdom-ordered). The ORIGINAL .7.4.6 derivation-CONSTRUCTION idea (bounded, no-search) IS the real lever for the residual → .7.4.6.3.`
  Commit: `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0147`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.4.6.3`
  Status: `done` (code, PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0148, 2026-06-04; witness-pass PROVEN 146/150 — gate residual measurement in flight)
  Goal: `Replace witness SEARCH with derivation-directed CONSTRUCTION (the original .7.4.6 idea — confirmed correct after .6.1/.6.2 showed caching is insufficient): build the minimal derivation tree to each target directly, always expanding the shortest-terminating production (the .7.4.2 compute_min_terminal_lengths table gives the per-node minimum), top-down, BACKTRACK-FREE. A constructed witness completes in O(tree-size) — NO search, NO timeout — bridging the seconds->budget gap that caching cannot. For branch targets, force the branch via the reach plan; for everything else, expand shortest-terminating. WHY construction beats caching: the ~75% generate_node/rule/sequence self-time is the SEARCH (deep + backtracking), which caching (per-step) can't remove but construction (minimal tree, no backtrack) eliminates. Design slice first (where it plugs into generate_target_witnesses), then implement; measure residual 273 -> ~0 via canonical gate; MONOTONE (witness-pass-only).`
  Acceptance: `constructed witnesses complete in bounded time (no timeout); canonical gate residual 273 -> ~0 (or honest irreducible remainder); MONOTONE; determinism; lib+clippy green.`
  Verification: `done (code, PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0148, 2026-06-04) — implemented construct_mode (a SUB-mode of witness_mode, never active in the diverse/default pass → monotone). FIVE edits in stimuli_generator.rs: (B) construct_mode: bool field; (C) init false in new(); (A) generate_or witness arm — after building 'ordered' (alts sorted by ascending .7.4.2 min-terminal-length), 'if self.construct_mode { ordered.truncate(1); }' → commit to the SINGLE shortest-terminating branch, NO backtrack; (D) generate_quantified — 'if self.construct_mode { vec![min_repeat] }' → emit the minimum legal repeat count; (E) generate_target_witnesses tries construction FIRST (construct_mode=true; bounded O(tree-size), no search) and falls back to the .7.4.5 search on Err; function-end resets construct_mode=false. Reuses ALL existing validity machinery (spacing/terminals/round-trip guards) — only the CHOICE is forced. WITNESS-PASS PROOF (150-sample, 7000ms, seed 712001): resolved 0->146/150 (was 124 search / 81 original; +22), target_timeout=1 (was 23-55 — near-eliminated), 31.4s — construction makes deeply-factored witnesses BOUNDED + fast, exactly the seconds->budget bridge caching could not provide. MONOTONE BY CONSTRUCTION: witness pass is additive (residual can only shrink), falls back to search on construction failure, diverse pass byte-identical. lib (no-features) 588/588; source-strict clippy 0. CANONICAL GATE LANDED (PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0149, 2026-06-04, make sv_stimuli_quality_gate, exit 0, ~22min): closed_loop_replay_targets_total 273 -> 97 (-64%); FULL ARC ~2660 initial -> 888 (.7.2) -> 753 (.7.4.4 Purdom) -> 273 (.7.4.5 budget) -> 97 (construction). Realistic corpus 730/730 PASS; declared_shadow 16/16; parse_full_failures 0. NOT literal-0: the 97 residual is dominated by ~66 target_timeout (replay witness passes 2017+2023 = 33+33) — construction dead-ends that fall back to the .7.4.5 SEARCH and time out at the gate's 200ms floor. SV main parser stays Mostly Done (focused_replay_target_debt_zero unmet at 97). NEXT (.7.4.6.4, WHY+WHERE first): diagnose the ~66 dead-end targets — reduce construction's fall-through to search (bounded limited-backtrack construction: on a dead-end, try next-shortest sibling rather than full search) so the residual approaches literal-0.`
  Commit: `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0148 (code) + PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0149 (gate residual 97)`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.7.4.6.5`
  Status: `done` (code, PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0150, 2026-06-04; 2x construction speedup, gate residual confirm in flight)
  Goal: `Eliminate the construction allocation churn pinned by .7.4.6.4: strip_probability_prefix cloned every OR alternative's subtree on every generate_or call (the probability prefix is RARE, so the clone is almost always wasted). Make it return Cow<ASTNode> — borrow in the common no-prefix case, own only the rare stripped case. Parser-agnostic (generic ASTNode), monotone (identical stripped node → byte-identical generation), benefits EVERY parser's generation (the user's speed phase: functionality+accuracy THEN speed).`
  Acceptance: `byte-identical generation (determinism preserved); measurable construction speedup; gate residual does not regress (<= 97; monotone-improving at the bounded budget since faster construction can only fit MORE in budget); lib+clippy green; zero grammar identifiers.`
  Verification: `done (code) — strip_probability_prefix returns (Option<u32>, Cow<ASTNode>): Cow::Borrowed(node) for the common no-probability-prefix case, Cow::Owned(stripped) only when a prefix is present; generate_or's prepared vec holds Cow; all read sites use .as_ref(); the chosen branch still clones once (unchanged). MEASURED: 150-sample witness pass @7000ms wall 31.4s -> 14.8s (2.1x faster), resolution byte-identical (146/150 unchanged). MONOTONE: diverse pass + unbounded witness byte-identical across runs (--output cmp). lib 588/588; clippy source 0. NOTE: does NOT by itself reach literal-0 — property_expr deep branches are ~3s even at 2x, still > the gate's 200ms budget (honest-irreducible at that budget); the Cow win is a real all-parser speed deliverable + monotone-improving for the gate. CANONICAL GATE (residual <= 97 + faster runtime) measurement in flight.`
  Commit: `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0150 (gate confirm amended on landing)`

Plan: (1) trace the branch-coverage mechanism for the 4 to pin (B)'s WHY; (2) decide (A) the gate-budget tradeoff (raise floor vs gate runtime); (3) for proven-unreachable (B) branches, either fix the reachability classifier or flag via the shadowing lint.`

  (1) "branch targets enumerated PRE-LR-transform don't map to the POST-LR parser (phantom)" = DISPROVEN. transform_from_raw_ast (mod.rs:1355-1533) APPLIES eliminate_left_recursive_patterns at :1499-1500 (config.eliminate_left_recursion defaults true) BEFORE returning grammar_tree; the stimuli generator (main.rs:1083) and the parser both consume that SAME post-LR-transform tree.   Also confirmed: the universe builder (stimuli_generator.rs:2075-2214) only pushes branch_reachable targets (:2205); a branch referencing a missing rule is marked unreachable (:2116) and NOT pushed — so branch-level missing-rule phantoms are already excluded by construction.
  => The residual is GENUINE non-coverage. And the counters are the STIMULI GENERATOR'S, not the parser's: record_branch_selected fires at the TOP of the generator's ordered-choice attempt loop (:10302, inside generate_or_*, immediately before "Trying OR branch: ..."), while record_branch_success fires only where a branch body actually generates (:10350, :10382, :10435, :10535). ⇒ selected_but_failed means THE GENERATOR TRIED TO EMIT THIS BRANCH AND COULD NOT.   MEASURED DISTRIBUTION (canonical run's new manifest, closed_loop_replay_targets.json): 81 of 83 residual targets are selected_but_failed; the other 2 are never_hit (the class-C store-gated rule pair). ZERO never_selected -- consistent with .7.4.6.11 having eliminated class B entirely.

  (2) THEREFORE TWO OF THE GOAL'S THREE CANDIDATES ARE IN THE WRONG SUBSYSTEM. Candidate (i) "PEG ordered-choice shadowing -- an earlier alternative always matches first" and candidate (ii) "the post-transform PARSER never selects that branch index" are both statements about the PARSER. The defect is measured on the GENERATOR side, so neither can be the cause of a selected_but_failed target. (Candidate (i) was independently shaky: TOOLBOX Protocol D states PGEN's | is a branch TOURNAMENT, default longest_match, NOT PEG first-match commit.) Candidate (iii), generator depth/recursion exhaustion, is the ONLY survivor -- and it is the only one in the right subsystem.

  (2) WHAT IT SAYS, READ VERBATIM. profile_2017_replay_gap.json, branch::prop_primary_sv_2017::root#9 (selected=48, success=0):
    46  Stimuli generation helper timeout exceeded for rule 'prop_primary_sv_2017' at path 'root/o9' (budget=1000ms)

  (3) WHY EVERY PASS-LEVEL COUNTER STILL READS 0 -- THE .7.4.6.8 PARADOX ("62 unresolved against exactly 1 recorded failure") RESOLVED. Meanwhile generate_or's reach-forcing arm puts the forced branch first and keeps every sibling as a fallback -- its own comment says why: "the remaining branches follow in natural order as fallbacks (so generation still terminates if the forced branch fails downstream)" (:10019-10022). ⇒ forced branch #9 depth-fails, sibling #0 (sequence_expr) succeeds, the rule returns Ok, the target is silently uncredited, and construct_fell_back_to_search does not tick either (it counts only whole-target construct failures). The counters are STRUCTURALLY incapable of seeing this class. Promoted: [[branch-failure-reasons-are-the-witness-why]].

  (4) ⭐ THE QUANTITATIVE MECHANISM -- IT IS NOT "THE TARGET IS TOO DEEP". Two static instruments mirror the engine's own recursion accounting (generate_rule fails at depth > max_depth :9491; body at d+1 :9740; rule_reference at d+1 :11347; Quantified element at d+1 :11757; Or/Sequence pass depth THROUGH unchanged :10375/:10693; entry at 0 :9163) and its own Purdom table (min_terminal_length_of_node :7639, ordering :10054-10079, construct_mode's `ordered.truncate(1)` :10087):
    * purdom_depth = the derivation construct_mode ACTUALLY commits to (min TERMINAL LENGTH at every Or, tie by index, no fallback inside the committed path);
    * min_depth    = the shallowest derivation that exists at all.
  MEASURED, prop_primary_sv_2017 (identical in sv_2023): COVERED #0/#1/#2 purdom_depth=34, #28 purdom_depth=18 -- all <= 40. RESIDUAL {3..27,29} purdom_depth=48 (50 for #10) -- all > 40, while their min_depth is 30-32, i.e. COMFORTABLY INSIDE the existing budget. ⇒ THE BUDGET SITS BETWEEN THE TWO. Purdom SHORT minimizes tokens, not levels; the shortest-in-tokens property_expr descends the property-operator ladder and then the expression precedence cascade, 8-14 levels past a budget a shallower derivation would have fitted inside. Population-wide over the whole residual: 79 branches, BUDGET=79, STRUCTURAL=0, purdom_depth 42-54 vs min_depth 18-36.

  (5) GROUND TRUTH INSIDE BOTH INSTRUMENTS, and one of them REFUSED TWICE before it was trusted ([[feedback_instrument_needs_ground_truth]]). Controls replay the canonical run's own measured split. Per-rule: positive 4/4 (COVERED), negative 26/26 (residual) -- exact, both profiles. Population-wide: negative 79/79, positive 104/104. ⛔ THE REFUSALS WERE BOTH REAL DEFECTS IN THE INSTRUMENT, NOT NOISE: (a) a first version charged depth only on the SHALLOWEST derivation and reported residual branches needing 30-32 <= 40 -- 26 negative-control misses, which is what forced the Purdom-vs-shallow distinction that turned out to BE the mechanism; (b) the population version then missed on property_case_item#1 in BOTH profiles -- a branch that is COVERED yet has purdom_depth=48. Its coverage record explains it and confirms the model: selected=2 success=1 with one recorded max_depth=40 failure, i.e. it lost construct_mode exactly as predicted and was rescued by the SEARCH fallback (which does not truncate the attempt order). The control was re-stated against what the model actually claims -- construct-mode failure, evidenced by the branch's own recorded max_depth=40 entry -- and the 8 search-rescued branches are printed BY NAME rather than excluded.

TWO WITNESS PASSES, ONE CAPABILITY -- the same installer asymmetry class .7.4.6.11 closed for quantifier forcing, and the one .7.4.6.12 predicted for class C.

The +4 depth-slack retry (target_branch_depth_retry_slack :12072) should, on paper, fire at every residual branch site -- its three gates all hold there (the error IS depth exhaustion, the branch HAS a target deficit, success_hits IS 0).   FIX_PLAN_2026-08-01: `⭐ SIZED, and it is a WIRING change, not a design. Give generate_target_witnesses the per-target depth budget the CERT-COVERAGE witness pass already has:
  * compute min_derivation_depths = self.compute_min_full_derivation_depths() ONCE at :5473 (beside the existing max_depth/max_rule_visits slack), the same call the plannable pass makes at :4973;
  * replace the flat self.config.max_depth = original_max_depth.saturating_mul(2) (:5477) with a PER-TARGET budget set inside the loop: original_max_depth*2 + min_derivation_depths[status.rule_name], mirroring :4985's reach_prefix + min_subtree[target];
  * restore the original after each target (the pass already restores at :5664 -- keep that as the outer restore).
  WHY IT IS ADDITIVE-ONLY: min_full_derivation_depth is >= 0, so every budget only ever GROWS -- a target that witnesses today witnesses identically tomorrow. Non-witness surfaces never enter this function, so the diverse pass stays byte-identical and determinism is untouched. Predicted movement (from the population instrument): prop_primary needs 48 and would get 40 + min_full_derivation_depth[prop_primary_sv_2017]; the containers need 52-54 and their own subtree depths are larger still.
  ⛔ ITEM (a) HAS ALREADY BEEN MEASURED, AND IT CORRECTED THE PLAN -- USE VARIANT B, NOT THE FORMULA VERBATIM. Item (a) asked whether the resulting budget actually clears the required 48-54 rather than assuming it. Measured with the engine's OWN over-estimating proxy (min_full_derivation_depth_of_node :7714 + its fixpoint :7920, mirrored exactly) over all 40 profile_2017 class-A targets:
    * VARIANT A -- the .5.2 formula VERBATIM, `40 + min_full_derivation_depth[status.rule_name]`: clears **37/40**. It FAILS on checker_or_generate_item_declaration (needs 54, gets 47), package_or_generate_item_declaration_sv_2017 (needs 54, gets 47) and statement_item_sv_2017 (needs 52, gets 50).
    * WHY IT FAILS, and it is a REAL flaw not a rounding miss: `min_full_derivation_depth[rule]` is the depth of the rule's SHALLOWEST derivation. Those three are container rules with a very shallow minimum (proxy 7, 7, 10) whose RESIDUAL BRANCH is nowhere near the minimum. The .5.2 formula was designed for the cert-coverage pass, whose targets are RULES; a BRANCH target needs the depth of THAT BRANCH'S subtree.
    * VARIANT B -- branch-scoped, `40 + min_full_derivation_depth_of_node(alternative[branch_index]) + 1` for a Branch target (falling back to variant A for a Rule target): clears **40/40**, with margins of 5-38.
  ⇒ IMPLEMENT VARIANT B. Re-run the preview after any change: `python3 docs/tasks/artifacts/sv_exh_proof/class_a_fix_budget_preview.py`.
  ⛔ STILL TO MEASURE, DO NOT ASSUME: (b) whether the .5.5 two-tier escalation is needed here too or tier 1 suffices; (c) COST -- a deeper budget makes each witness a larger generation, so re-price the .7.4.5 two-tier witness budget and the .7.4.6.3 construct path, exactly the sizing .7.4.6.12 flagged for its own entry-policy change.
  ⛔ AND WHEN IT LANDS, LOWER THE PINS: closed_loop.replay_target_ceilings is a TWO-SIDED ratchet (.7.4.6.10) -- the gate FAILS on an unbanked win, by design.`
  FIX_LANDED_2026-08-01: `⭐ THE WIRING CHANGE, EXACTLY AS SIZED -- one new private helper plus three lines at the call site, all inside generate_target_witnesses. rust/src/ast_pipeline/stimuli_generator.rs is the ONLY source file touched.
  * `original_max_depth * 2` is no longer THE budget; it is `reach_prefix_budget`, the reach-prefix ALLOWANCE, and it stays the value in force outside the per-target loop (so every non-generation use is byte-identical).
  * `compute_min_full_derivation_depths()` is computed ONCE for the pass -- the same call the cert-coverage plannable pass makes -- and read per target.
  * NEW `witness_target_depth_budget(status, reach_prefix, depths)`: for a BRANCH target it resolves the Or site via the existing `or_alternatives_for_group_path(rule, node_path)`, indexes the TARGETED alternative, and returns `reach_prefix + min_full_derivation_depth_of_node(alternative) + 1` (the `+1` = the Or site's own level, mirroring what `min_full_derivation_depth_of_node` charges an Or node). A RULE target -- or a branch whose alternative cannot be located, or whose depth the fixpoint never resolved -- falls back to the `.5.2` rule-scoped formula. `bypass_fuel` became per-target too (`budget + 1`), matching the cert pass.
  ⭐ WHY VARIANT B AND NOT THE `.5.2` FORMULA VERBATIM -- the correction is REAL, not cosmetic: `min_derivation_depths[rule]` is the depth of the rule`s SHALLOWEST alternative, which is precisely the alternative a residual BRANCH target is not. It clears 37/40 and under-budgets three CONTAINER rules with a shallow minimum and a deep residual branch. The landed formula is BYTE-FOR-BYTE the one the preview instrument measured at 40/40, so what shipped is what was measured (`python3 docs/tasks/artifacts/sv_exh_proof/class_a_fix_budget_preview.py` -- re-run at implementation time, unchanged: A 37/40, B 40/40).
  ADDITIVE BY CONSTRUCTION, NOT BY ARGUMENT: both addends are `>= 0`, so every budget only ever GROWS relative to the flat one -- no target that witnessed before can stop witnessing. Nothing outside this pass reads the per-target budget, so the diverse pass stays byte-identical and determinism is untouched. Zero grammar identifiers; it reads derivation structure only.
  +1 unit test, `witness_target_depth_budget_is_branch_scoped_not_rule_scoped`, pinning all four claims on a 4-rule synthetic grammar: the deep branch out-budgets the rule-scoped formula, the budget equals `reach_prefix + depth(branch) + Or level` exactly, NO budget ever falls below the flat `reach_prefix` (the monotonicity floor), and an unresolvable branch path falls back to the rule-scoped value.`
  ANSWERS_TO_THE_FIX_PLAN_OPEN_ITEMS_2026-08-01: `The FIX_PLAN left (b) and (c) explicitly "STILL TO MEASURE, DO NOT ASSUME". Both are now measured, and neither needed a design change.
  * (b) IS THE `.5.5` TWO-TIER ESCALATION NEEDED HERE? **NO -- tier 1 suffices, and there is a structural reason, confirmed by measurement.** `.5.5`s second tier exists for a deep MANDATORY OFF-PATH SIBLING encountered while descending a reach path from a DIFFERENT entry rule. This pass roots every witness at the target`s OWN rule, so there is no entry-to-target prefix to carry off-path siblings: the only mandatory siblings are inside the forced alternative`s own subtree, and `min_full_derivation_depth_of_node` already charges a `Sequence` its DEEPEST element. Measurement agrees -- single-tier variant B resolved 40/40 of the class-A targets on the live engine, with nothing left for a tier 2 to rescue.
  * (c) COST: **it got CHEAPER, which is the opposite of what the plan feared.** Same-depth A/B, same machine, same seed: 612 s -> 571 s (-7%), peak_tree_rss 141 MB (guard exit 0). The deeper budget does NOT make each witness a bigger generation in practice, because the pass previously PAID the deep descent and then threw it away: it exhausted the budget, errored, and re-ran the whole target through the search fallback. `construct_fell_back_to_search` 12 -> 1 is that waste disappearing. ⛔ Recorded as a REVERSED expectation, not smoothed over -- the plan predicted a cost and the measurement found a saving.`

  * generate_target_witnesses takes the RUN's entry_rule (main.rs passes resolved_entry_rule -- the same entry the target report's reachability universe is defined against; None keeps the canonical rule_order[0]). Resolved ONCE per pass.
  * NEW private verdict witness_target_is_store_entry_blocked(status): does the target's MANDATORY rendering force a POSITIVE store gate whose kind NO rule in the target rule's own reachable closure can @emit_fact? When yes -- and only then -- the witness generates from the raised entry with set_reach_plan_for_rule installed, which brings the hops, the quantifier forcing AND compute_reach_prelude's producer prelude. Everything else keeps the own-rule entry byte-for-byte.
  * NEW private emittable_kinds_within_closure(rule): the kinds the target's own subtree could ever emit. Deliberately an OVER-approximation (ignores render order + optionality), so the verdict is biased toward NOT raising -- a missed raise leaves prior behaviour untouched, a spurious one would replace a working generation.
  * NEW StoreGateScope enum threaded through mandatory_reach_gate / mandatory_node_gated. The pre-existing reach-BFS caller passes AnyQuery => byte-identical.
  ⭐ THREE SCOPING RULES, EACH PREVENTING A NAMED FAILURE (each pinned by the unit test, with its negative control):
    (a) POSITIVE gates only (gen_count_kinds + gen_name_gate, never reach_gate_kinds). A lacks_fact gate is SATISFIED by the empty store a standalone witness starts with; counting it would raise the entry for targets that witness fine today. ⛔ The reach-BFS deprioritization counts every fact query ON PURPOSE -- there over-counting only re-ranks two ways of generating the same thing. A verdict that CHANGES what is generated cannot borrow that scope.
    (b) MANDATORY descent, not "a gate exists below here". An Or with an ungated alternative is an escape the generator simply takes. MEASURED: the first version of the independent instrument used a whole-closure scan and called net_declaration_sv_2017 BLOCKED; the mandatory walk correctly does not (its alternative 0 is a plain `wire a;`). That miscalibration is now the instrument's own discriminating control.
    (c) CLOSURE-relative, not absolute. The same gate on the same rule is NOT blocking when a producer for its kind lives inside the target's own subtree.
  * A BRANCH target is judged on its TARGETED ALTERNATIVE first (the rule's root Or almost always offers an ungated escape -- which is exactly why a whole-rule walk cannot see a gate only the forced branch reaches), then on the whole rule.
  ⭐ RULE-TARGET-ONLY, AND THAT IS A DECISION, NOT AN OVERSIGHT -- see HONEST_BOUND_2026-08-02.

  FIX_PRICED_AND_BLOCKED_2026-08-02 (`PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0172`, the TOOL-BUILD + the A/B): `⭐⭐⭐ THE ROOT CAUSE IS NOT THE +4 ARITHMETIC. It is a MISSING RUNAWAY BACKSTOP, and the asymmetry is one screenful wide: generate_or's Err arm holds TWO retries, and only one of them is bounded.

  * should_reach_retry_uncovered_recursive (the constructive-reach sibling) has been bounded by MAX_UNCOVERED_REACH_RETRIES=4096 since GRAMMAR-WELLFORMED.H.4.2, with a doc comment explaining exactly why a retry needs a backstop.

  ⛔⛔ CORRECTION, MEASURED AFTER THE A/B AND RECORDED RATHER THAN QUIETLY DROPPED (the .7.4.6.4/.6/.9/.12 precedent): THIS LEAF HOLDS **TWO** DEFECTS, NOT ONE, AND THE BACKSTOP FIXES ONLY THE FIRST.

(i) needs the per-branch backstop; (ii) still needs its own bound -- the Goal's OTHER candidate, slack relative to the ORIGINAL configured max_depth, or a nesting cap. ⛔ DO NOT let the backstop landing be read as closing this leaf's Goal: the Goal asks for (ii).

New read-only DepthSlackRetryCensus (stimuli_generator.rs) records, per NESTING LEVEL, attempts/successes/max_budget, and per BRANCH the retry ORDINAL at which each success landed. Printed as one `Depth-slack retry census:` line, only when the retry fired.

  THE CAP IS READ OFF THE DISTRIBUTION, NOT CURVE-FITTED. Pricing per-branch cap N against the measured success ordinals: N=64 keeps 214/255 + 147/147; N=1024 keeps 245/255 + 147/147; N=4096 keeps 252/255 + 147/147; N=131072 keeps all. 4096 is chosen because it retains 98.8%/100% AND is the magnitude its sibling backstop already uses -- a reader comparing the two retries should not have to wonder why their bounds disagree.

  | | sv_2017 | sv_2023 |
  | retry attempts | 1 964 056 -> 302 526 (6.5x less) | 2 959 658 -> 408 247 (7.2x less) |
  | retry successes | 255 -> 252 | 147 -> 398 |

  | witness store_entry_raises | 1 -> 0 | 1 -> 1 |

  Goal: `Give the RAISED WITNESS ENTRY (.7.4.6.12) the BRANCH-target half. Today the raise fires for RULE targets only; a store-entry-blocked BRANCH target is closed only TRANSITIVELY, because its blocking rule happens to be a residual rule target AND is referenced from exactly one site. TWO mechanisms are missing for the general case, and both are named: (1) compute_count_prelude / compute_name_prelude discover their gated rule by a MANDATORY descent from the target RULE, whose root Or has ungated escapes, so a branch target arms NO prelude -- the discovery must be able to start from the TARGETED ALTERNATIVE (the same branch-scoping .7.4.6.12's verdict already does via target_forces_positive_store_gate); (2) the branch installer set_reach_plan_mode never calls compute_reach_prelude at all, so even a correctly-discovered gate would not be attached -- and it must stay witness-pass-scoped, because set_reach_plan is the PRIMARY target-drive pass whose output must stay byte-identical.`
  PRIOR_ART_SIZING_2026-08-02 (`PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0172`, read before designing anything — [[feedback_read_prior_art_before_designing]]): `⭐ THE GOAL NAMED TWO MISSING MECHANISMS. ONE OF THEM IS ALREADY BUILT, THE OTHER IS SMALLER THAN IT LOOKS, AND A THIRD INGREDIENT THE GOAL DID NOT MENTION IS ALSO ALREADY BUILT. Read at the source before writing code:

  (a) ⭐ ALREADY BUILT -- THE BLOCKED VERDICT IS ALREADY BRANCH-AWARE. The Goal says discovery "must be able to start from the TARGETED ALTERNATIVE (the same branch-scoping .7.4.6.12's verdict already does via target_forces_positive_store_gate)". That function ALREADY judges a Branch target on its targeted alternative FIRST (or_alternatives_for_group_path -> mandatory_node_gated, StoreGateScope::PositiveOnly) and only then on the whole rule -- so witness_target_is_store_entry_blocked returns a correct verdict for a BRANCH target TODAY, with no change. Nothing to build here; the branch-scoping to copy is 8 lines and it is the model for (c).

  (b) ⭐ ALREADY BUILT -- THE BRANCH INSTALLER ALREADY TAKES A SEPARATE ENTRY RULE. set_reach_plan_forcing_quantifiers(entry_rule, target_rule, target_node_path, target_branch_index, bypass_fuel) has ALWAYS had two independent rule parameters, and compute_reach_path_with_quantifier_sites computes the entry->target path. The witness pass simply calls it with entry == target (&entry_rule, &entry_rule, ...) because it roots every witness at the target's own rule. ⇒ the raise for a branch target is a CALL-SITE change -- pass (raised, &status.rule_name, node_path, branch_index, bypass_fuel) -- not a new installer. The Rule-only guard on the raise arm is the whole of what excludes branch targets today.

  (c) ⛔ GENUINELY MISSING, AND IT IS THE REAL WORK -- THE PRELUDE. set_reach_plan_mode builds its plan and never sets plan.prelude, while the RULE path (install_reach_plan_from_hops) calls compute_reach_prelude. Without it a raised-entry branch witness steers down correctly and still fails the gate, because construct_mode renders the minimal derivation and never spontaneously emits the enabling declaration. AND compute_count_prelude / compute_name_prelude would find no gate even once wired: they locate gated_rule by scanning the HOP rules + target_rule, then fall back to count_gate_via_mandatory_descent(target_rule, 0) -- and net_declaration_sv_2017's root Or has ungated escapes, so the mandatory descent stops. ⇒ they need the branch-scoped descent start that (a) already implements one function over.

  ⇒ SIZE: two edits (a witness-scoped prelude leg on set_reach_plan_mode; a branch-scoped descent start on the two prelude builders) plus a call-site widening. ⛔ MONOTONICITY IS THE CONSTRAINT, NOT THE SIZE: set_reach_plan is the PRIMARY target-drive pass and its output must stay byte-identical, so the prelude leg must be a separate opt-in exactly as .7.4.6.11 split set_reach_plan_forcing_quantifiers out rather than changing set_reach_plan.`

- [x] **ROOT CAUSE (WHY + WHERE)** — the BRANCH-target reach plan carries **no semantic prelude**,
  so a raised branch witness steers correctly and still renders the gated rule against an empty
  store. **WHERE**, in three source facts read before any code was written (`-0173`): (1) the
  verdict `target_forces_positive_store_gate` is ALREADY branch-aware — it judges the targeted
  alternative first — so nothing was missing there; (2) `set_reach_plan_forcing_quantifiers` has
  ALWAYS taken independent `entry_rule`/`target_rule`, so the raise is a call-site widening, not a
  new installer; (3) `set_reach_plan_mode` never sets `plan.prelude` while the rule path
  (`install_reach_plan_from_hops`) calls `compute_reach_prelude` — and even once wired,
  `compute_count_prelude`'s discovery would find nothing, because its fallback
  `count_gate_via_mandatory_descent(target_rule, 0)` stops at `net_declaration_sv_2017`'s root `Or`,
  whose first alternative is the ungated `wire a;`. **WHY, proven on the real parser rather than
  argued** (`parseability_probe --parse systemverilog … --profile sv_2017`): the witness the fix
  produces, `import\foo ::*;package\foo ;\foo \foo ;endpackage`, gives `parse_full passed for
  grammar 'systemverilog'`; the byte-identical sample with only the leading import removed gives
  `Parser did not consume full input at position 0 [furthest_position=17, …]` — position 17 is the
  escaped net-type identifier, so it is the *upstream declaration* that the branch needs, exactly as
  for the rule half. And the sample is **absent from arm B's stimuli** (`grep -c` → 0) and present
  in arm C's at line 15664 — the prelude is what puts it there.
- [x] **FIX** — fix-hierarchy tier: **engine, minimal, capability-symmetry-restoring** (no grammar
  change, no new annotation surface, zero grammar identifiers outside the one real-grammar test).
  Three edits: (a) `generate_target_witnesses`'s raise arm drops its `Rule`-only guard and, for a
  branch target, records the raised entry for the branch arm to install; (b) new opt-in
  `set_reach_plan_forcing_quantifiers_with_prelude` — a separate entry point exactly as `.7.4.6.11`
  split `set_reach_plan_forcing_quantifiers` out of `set_reach_plan`, so the primary target-drive
  pass and the ordinary branch-witness path stay byte-identical; (c) branch-scoped gate discovery
  (`count_gate_via_targeted_alternative`, `name_gate_via_targeted_alternative`), tried **last** in
  both prelude builders so every gate the existing legs find is found identically. The name-gate leg
  keeps `.4b.7`'s "only when the render is unavoidably store-gated" guard, applied to the
  alternative node — without it a prelude could be armed on an alternative that renders fine from an
  empty store and BREAK a working witness. `compute_reach_path_with_quantifier_sites` now also
  returns its hop chain, so the prelude is built from the very path the plan steers instead of a
  second BFS. Fail-safe throughout: any miss falls back to the unchanged own-rule install.
