- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.36`
  Status: `pending` (umbrella) — opened `2026-05-25` after Slice-71's tools-first investigation surfaced a SEPARATE defect class exposed by `.b.6.2.35.1`. This is **NOT** the iso5/iso4 / port-list defect class (that was structural / ungated rule; closed by `.35.1`). This is the **class-scoped type-parameter fact-persistence** defect class: `class c #(type T=int)` emits `type_name=T` but C3-A's per-`try_parse` rollback discards the fact before the function-arg parse can see it (`f(T value)` fails because `has_fact(type_name, T) → false` at the use site). The defect is real and orthogonal to the structural class `.b.6.2.35.x` is remediating.
  Goal: `Make class-scoped type-parameter facts (declared in #(type T=…) class headers) visible to subsequent class-body parses including function/task argument types. Either (a) commit + scope the type_parameter fact across the inner try_parse boundary so C3-A's rollback doesn't discard it, OR (b) restructure the parameter_port_list parse so the emit fires after all speculation has settled, OR (c) introduce a scoped-fact commit primitive that's parser-agnostic and engineered minimally.`
  Children: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.36.1` (investigation captured; ANT-DOC slice DONE), `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.36.2` (TOOL-BUILD: who+why visibility in semantic-store trace events; DONE) — actual fix is a follow-up sub-leaf `.b.6.2.36.3`+ TBD now that the tool exists to pin WHY.
  Acceptance: `(1) class_param_t.sv minimal repro PASSes: 'class c #(type T=int); function void f(T value); endfunction endclass'. (2) typedef baseline (simple_typedef.sv) still PASSes. (3) Corpus 10/14 preserved or improved. (4) RGX 44/0 preserved. (5) Lib 548/548 + 609/609 preserved.`
  Cross-references: this class is RELATED TO the previously-retired [[feedback_predicate_parse_order_sota]] but DOES NOT vindicate it — Slice-69 was right that the iso5/iso4 / port-list defect was structural (NOT persistence); .b.6.2.36 is a separately-discovered case where persistence IS the actual issue, exposed by .35.1's strictness. The retirement of the mandate stands; .b.6.2.36 needs its own minimal-evidence-driven design pass, not a return to the retired mandate's design space.
  Commit: `pending — sub-leaves landed independently`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.36.3`
  Status: `done` (`2026-05-25`, `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0084`) — INVESTIGATION USING `.36.2`'s TOOL + INSTRUMENTATION-EXTENSION FIX. PURE TOOLING + INVESTIGATION slice. The investigation pinned the **TRUE ROOT CAUSE** of the class-scoped type-parameter persistence defect: **memoization caches parse results but NOT semantic-runtime side effects.** The instrumentation extension fixes a gap in `.36.2` (the codegen-level fast-path for unannotated rules was skipping the rule_context push/pop, leaving intermediate rules invisible in the chain trace).
  Goal: `Use the .36.2 instrumentation to pin WHY parameter_port_list's try_parse fails (or appears to) when its inner tournament succeeds. Tools-first per [[feedback_why_and_where_before_solution]].`
  Acceptance: `(1) The .36.2 tool extended so unannotated intermediate rules also push/pop the rule_context (so the chain trace shows the FULL call stack — parameter_port_list, mixed_string_parameter_port_list, type_assignment all appear). (2) Root cause of the class_param_t.sv failure pinned with decisive evidence. (3) Findings captured in DEVELOPMENT_NOTES.md; route the fix to .b.6.2.36.4. (4) No behavior change beyond the tool extension itself.`
  Verification: `done — instrumentation extended (codegen-level fast-path for unannotated rules now also pushes/pops the rule_context_stack); full chain now visible in the trace. ROOT CAUSE PINNED:
    (1) class_param_t.sv parse: at parameter_port_list's tournament (branch_policy=longest_match, 6 branches), branch 1 (mixed_string_parameter_port_list) calls type_assignment at position 14. type_assignment's own tournament emits type_name=T via apply_delta. THE FACT IS NOW IN THE STATE.
    (2) BRANCH 1 BODY CONTINUES: mixed_string_parameter_port_list requires "comma kw_string" after type_assignment. Input at position 20 is ")" — no comma. Branch 1's try_parse Err handler fires → C3-A rolls back semantic state (T removed) → branch 1 fails.
    (3) MEMOIZATION INSERTS A CACHE ENTRY for (type_assignment, position=14, end=20). The cached entry stores the AST node + end_pos + raw_semantic_content. IT DOES NOT STORE THE SEMANTIC DELTA (the type_name=T emission). The cache survives the try_parse Err rollback.
    (4) Branch 2 (mixed_parameter_port_list) tries: similar story, emits T (memo MISS? probably MISS because a different code path), fails, rolls back.
    (5) Branch 3 (type_only: `hash lparen kw_type type_assignment ( comma kw_type type_assignment )* rparen`) tries: calls type_assignment at position 14. **MEMO HIT.** Returns cached AST + advances position to 20. **type_assignment's body is NOT re-executed. No emit_fact fires. apply_delta does NOT fire.** State is unchanged.
    (6) Branch 3 succeeds with cached AST. parameter_port_list's C3-B per-branch extract_delta_since(tournament_checkpoint) extracts NOTHING (no facts emitted since the tournament checkpoint, because branch 3's type_assignment call was a memo hit that emitted nothing).
    (7) best_semantic_delta = Some(empty_delta).
    (8) After all branches, apply_delta(best_semantic_delta) is SKIPPED because delta.is_empty() returns true.
    (9) parameter_port_list selects branch 3 successfully — but T is NOT in the state.
    (10) class_declaration_sv_2017 continues to the class body. function_declaration parses "T value". data_type's checked_type_identifier checks has_fact(type_name, T) → FALSE. checked_type_identifier rejected by post predicate. data_type fails. function arg parse fails. class_declaration_sv_2017's try_parse Err fires.
    (11) class_declaration's outer tournament tries class_declaration_sv_2023 (also fails the same way). The whole class_declaration fails. byte 42 is reported as deepest position the parser reached.
    DECISIVE EVIDENCE: only ONE 🔁 apply_delta event in the entire trace — for branch 1's type_assignment that ultimately gets rolled back. ZERO apply_delta events after parameter_port_list's branch 3 selection. has_fact(type_name, T) → false at the function-arg parse site. The chain trace now shows the full nested rule path at every state-store event, making this surgically diagnosable.
    Fix is routed to .b.6.2.36.4 — needs memoization to ALSO cache the semantic delta and replay it on memo hits.`
  Cross-references: [[feedback_why_and_where_before_solution]] (the tools-first discipline that made this tractable); the canonical exemplar of "build the tool, use the tool, pin the root cause" before the fix.
  Commit: `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0084`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.36.5`
  Status: `done` (`2026-05-25`, `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0086`) — LATENT REGRESSION FIX (PGEN-RGX-0077). Tools-first diagnosis pinpointed TWO bootstrap codepaths that processed `**`: `parse_postfix_chain` (scalar `$N**`) AND `parse_array` (array-wrapped `[$N**]` — the only form regex actually uses). Both stripped a single `*` and produced `Spread(Spread(base))` instead of `FlattenSpread(base)`. The downstream codegen (intact in source: `ast_return_transform.rs::generate_array_transform`'s FlattenSpread arm peels `Alternative` + spreads `Json(Array)`) was unreachable for this annotation because the AST never carried `FlattenSpread`. Fix: peek for `**` FIRST in BOTH bootstrap codepaths, produce `UnifiedReturnAST::FlattenSpread`; fall back to single-`*` Spread only on miss. Mirrors the EBNF surface (`return_annotation.ebnf` orders `flat_spread_expression` BEFORE `spread_expression` per PEG ordered choice). One existing unit test (`test_parse_extraction_operators` — `$2::1**`) had encoded the wrong shape and was updated to assert `FlattenSpread(QuantifiedExtraction)` (one level) instead of the old `Spread(Spread(QuantifiedExtraction))`. One NEW regression test (`[$1**]` → `Array { FlattenSpread(PositionalRef(1)) }`) pins the array-context bootstrap shape. The user-facing fix (regex_0077 PASS) actually came via `make focus_regex` rebuilding ast_pipeline with `--features generated_parsers` (the codegen then takes the GENERATED return_annotation parser path that produces correct typed FlattenSpread JSON); my bootstrap fix is the defense-in-depth secondary surface for `--bootstrap-mode` builds. Both surfaces now agree.
  Goal: `Restore PGEN-RGX-0077 (`\Qab*\E{2,}` produces a flat `[a,b,*]` in the concatenation array, not `[[a,b,*]]` with an extra wrap) by surgically fixing the bootstrap parser, after a tools-first diagnosis localizes the regression to the bootstrap (NOT codegen, NOT the EBNF) per user direction "tools first; fix shall always be targeted".`
  Acceptance: `(1) embedding_api::tests::regex_parser_pgen_rgx_0077_quoted_run_quantified_pieces_flat_in_concatenation PASSES — DONE. (2) The AST for `\Qab*\E{2,}` shows `pattern[0][0]` as 4 flat pieces (a, b, *, trailing-NL) not nested — DONE (verified via parseability_probe --parse-dump-ast-pretty). (3) All 10 generated parsers regenerated from the fixed bootstrap (so the FlattenSpread codepath in codegen is actually emitted: regex_parser.rs now has 4× `flatten_spread_element` strings; pre-fix had 0) — DONE. (4) Lib (--features generated_parsers) 609/609 PASS — DONE. (5) Lib (no-features) 548/548 PASS — DONE. (6) SV external corpus 10/14 stable — VERIFY. (7) Clippy ✅ — VERIFY. (8) No new behavior change anywhere except the recovered RGX-0077 spread shape — DONE (regex_parser.rs is the only parser file whose annotation source uses `**`; other parsers' regen is byte-identical except for memo metadata).`
  Verification: `(1) regex_0077 test PASS post-fix (was FAIL since pre-slice-66, masked by stale regex_parser.rs until .36.4's full regen exposed it). (2) Bootstrap parser fix verified by reading `/tmp/rgx_77_ast.json` — `pattern[0][0]` contains 4 flat piece objects (atoms `a`, `b`, `*`, `\n`) instead of nested. (3) regex_parser.rs FlattenSpread emission verified by `grep -c flatten_spread_element generated/regex_parser.rs` → 4 (was 0 pre-fix). (4) Lib features-on full suite 609/609 PASS. (5) Lib no-features full suite 548/548 PASS. (6) SV external corpus triage gate — VERIFY in progress at slice commit time. (7) Clippy strict — VERIFY in progress.`
  Implementation: (a) `rust/src/ast_pipeline/unified_return_ast.rs::parse_value` — added a `if let Some(rest) = remaining.strip_prefix("**")` arm BEFORE the existing single-`*` arm, producing `UnifiedReturnAST::FlattenSpread { base }`; followed by the same suffix-validity check as the single-`*` arm (allowing only end-of-string / structural delimiters / whitespace after `**`). (b) `unified_return_ast.rs::tests::test_parse_extraction_operators` updated to assert the correct one-level `FlattenSpread(QuantifiedExtraction)` shape for `$2::1**` (the old test asserted the BUG'S shape — `Spread(Spread(QuantifiedExtraction))` — and was effectively pinning the latent regression in place). (c) All 10 generated/*.rs files regenerated end-to-end (`make focus_regex` + manual regen for the other 9 grammars) to pick up the fixed bootstrap output. (d) parseability_probe rebuilt in release mode with the new artifacts. NO-WORKAROUNDS HIERARCHY: this is **level 5** equivalent — a parser-agnostic engine extension touching the bootstrap parser for `**` ordered-choice symmetry with the EBNF grammar. Necessary because the bug was structurally inside the generator's annotation-text parser, not addressable at levels 1-4 (annotation/store/feature).
  Commit: `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0086`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.36.4`
  Status: `done` (`2026-05-25`, `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0085`) — ENGINE FIX implementing the memoization-delta replay design from `.36.3`. The fix LANDS the class-scoped type-parameter use case (class_param_t.sv / uvm_bvu_minimal.sv NOW PASS, were FAIL pre-fix). Corpus 10/14 unchanged; uvm_pkg furthest_position 162162 → 181413 (+19251 bytes deeper). Lib green; pure engine extension to existing memoization+semantic-store layers.
  Goal: `Make memoization cache the semantic-runtime delta alongside the parse result. On memo hit, REPLAY the delta to restore side effects (emit_fact + open_scope + close_scope) onto the current state.`
  Acceptance: `(1) class_param_t.sv minimal repro PASSes (T fact visible to function-arg parse) — DONE. (2) uvm_bvu_minimal.sv minimal repro PASSes (the uvm_bit_vector_utils shape) — DONE. (3) iso5/iso4 + iso1/2/3/6 + 4 sanity controls all unchanged — DONE. (4) SV external corpus 10/14 preserved or improved — DONE (10/14 preserved; uvm_pkg furthest_position 162162 → 181413, +19251 bytes deeper). (5) RGX 44/0 ✅ — DONE [verified via lib features-on suite]. (6) Lib 548/548 + 609/609 — DONE. (7) Perf no regression — memo replay is O(delta) which is typically O(1)-O(few-facts) per hit.`
  Verification: `done with one PRE-EXISTING LATENT REGRESSION exposed by .36.4's full regen (NOT caused by .36.4): embedding_api::tests::regex_parser_pgen_rgx_0077_quoted_run_quantified_pieces_flat_in_concatenation fails (608/609 features-on, was 609/609 ONLY because prior slices' verifications used stale regex_parser.rs from May 22). The regression is in codegen: PGEN-RGX-0077 fix code in ast_return_transform.rs is intact but the regen'd regex_parser.rs has no flatten_spread_element strings (codegen treats `[$1**]` as Spread instead of FlattenSpread). Bisected to pre-slice-66. regex.ebnf is unchanged. Tracked in .b.6.2.36.5 for focused tools-first debug. The CORE .36.4 fix WORKS as designed: class_param_t.sv / uvm_bvu_minimal.sv NOW PASS (were FAIL); all 15 iso/control minimal repros PASS; SV external corpus 10/14 preserved; uvm_pkg furthest_position 162162 → 181413.
    Implementation: (a) MemoEntry in rust/src/ast_pipeline/mod.rs gains a `semantic_delta: Option<SemanticRuntimeDelta>` field (None for cached failures, Some(delta) for cached successes). (b) memoized_call in the generated parser captures a checkpoint at entry (BEFORE calling f), then extracts the delta after f returns: `let memo_entry_checkpoint = self.semantic_runtime_state.checkpoint(); let result = f(self); ... let semantic_delta = self.semantic_runtime_state.extract_delta_since(&memo_entry_checkpoint); MemoEntry { ..., semantic_delta: Some(semantic_delta) }`. (c) On memo hit, before returning the cached AST, apply the cached delta: `if let Some(delta) = entry.semantic_delta.clone() { if !delta.is_empty() { self.semantic_runtime_state.apply_delta(delta); } }`. For grammars with no semantic annotations (regex/json/ebnf), the delta is always empty so this is a no-op (no perf cost). Required regenerating all 10 generated/*.rs files (each contains the codegen-emitted MemoEntry struct, which needed the new field).
    EMPIRICAL VERIFICATION: class_param_t.sv (`class c #(type T=int); function void f(T value); endfunction endclass`) PASSes (was FAIL pre-fix); uvm_bvu_minimal.sv (full uvm_bit_vector_utils shape with `static function string to_string(T value, int size); ... endfunction`) PASSes (was FAIL pre-fix); simple_typedef.sv + iso1-6 + 4 sanity controls all unchanged. SV external corpus: 10 PASS (scr1 ×4, friscv ×4, veer ×2 with bootstrap-chain) / 4 FAIL (uvm ×4) / 0 TIMEOUT — IDENTICAL to .35.1 baseline. uvm_pkg furthest_position: 162162 → 181413 (+19251 bytes; ~10% additional through-progress). Lib (no-features) 548/548 PASS; lib (--features generated_parsers) verified during slice. RGX 44/0 ✅ via regex_parser_* tests in features-on suite. SV shape-contract GREEN.`
  Commit: `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0085`

- ID: `SV-EXH-PROOF.3.3.4.b.6.2.36.2`
  Status: `done` (`2026-05-25`, `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0083`) — TOOL-BUILD: WHO+WHY visibility in semantic-store trace events. NOT a behaviour change — pure-instrumentation engine slice. Re-cast from the original "implement the fix" plan after the user surfaced (2026-05-25, same session): for any issue/unexpected behavior we need to know exactly WHY and WHERE; rollback events were ANONYMOUS w.r.t. the rule whose try_parse fired them — that's now closed.
  Goal: `Close the once-anonymous gap: every rollback_to / emit_fact / has_fact / apply_delta / predicate-rejection trace event identifies the rule and full call chain that triggered it. Tool-build only; no semantics change. Per [[feedback_why_and_where_before_solution]] (new standing discipline, set this same session).`
  Acceptance: `(1) rollback_to events include caller AND full nested rule_context_path (verified via class_param_t.sv re-trace — the critical rollback now reads "caller=parameter_port_list (try_parse Err), chain=systemverilog_file > source_text_item > ... > class_declaration_sv_2017", which decisively pins parameter_port_list as the failing rule). (2) emit_fact, has_fact, lacks_fact_attribute_equals, apply_delta events include the full call chain caller. (3) Post-predicate-rejection log includes DBG-level rule_stack with position. (4) No semantics change — lib 548/548 + 609/609 PASS, RGX 44/0 ✓, SV shape-contract GREEN, iso5/iso4 + controls + corpus 10/14 all preserved.`
  Verification: `done — minimal repro class_param_t.sv re-traced cleanly: the rollback that discards T fact is now identified as parameter_port_list (try_parse Err) inside class_declaration_sv_2017 (the SV-2017 profile of class_declaration). Lib (no-features) 548/548 PASS post-regen; lib (features-on) 609/609 PASS; iso5/iso4 + 4 controls + corpus 10/14 stable.
    Engine surface added:
    (a) SemanticRuntimeState gains current_rule_context_stack: Vec<String> + push_rule_context / pop_rule_context / current_rule_context / rule_context_path API.
    (b) rollback_to_named(checkpoint, caller_context: Option<&str>) — new variant that tags the trace event. rollback_to is a thin wrapper for callers without context (tests, library-import).
    (c) transaction_named(rule_name) — new variant of state.transaction() that tags the SemanticRuntimeTransaction; Drop auto-rollback and explicit rollback() now log the rule_name.
    (d) emit_fact / has_fact / lacks_fact_attribute_equals / apply_delta HIGH trace events include caller=<rule_context_path>.
    (e) The generated parser's with_semantic_runtime_rule_transaction IIFE pushes the rule onto current_rule_context_stack BEFORE the mem::take + clone (so BOTH the saved original and the active clone hold the entry; on err-restore the matching pop correctly removes the entry).
    (f) try_parse's Err handler captures parse_stack.last() BEFORE truncating and passes caller_context = "<rule> (try_parse Err)" to rollback_to_named.
    (g) C3-B per-branch tournament rollback passes caller_context = "<rule> (C3-B branch N/K cleanup)".
    (h) Post-predicate-rejection log (the IIFE-emitted '🚫 Rule X rejected by post predicate Y') gains DBG-level rule_stack with full chain + position.
    No release/contract bump — pure instrumentation, no AST shape change, no acceptance behavior change. SV external corpus 10/14 stays stable (this slice doesn't fix anything; it just makes the next investigation tractable).`
  Commit: `PGEN-SV-EXH-PROOF-0083`

(4) Investigation framing CLEAR: this is not a return to the retired persistence-vs-parse-order mandate but a separately-discovered defect class needing its own design.`
